热木星与短周期行星1 篇

Mapping Stellar Obliquity Across Space and Time

Lauren I. Biddle, Brendan P. Bowler · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过比较114颗类太阳星的恒星倾角与行星轨道倾角,跨越原行星盘、碎片盘、温木星和热木星四个阶段和轨道距离,系统地探测恒星自转角动量与行星轨道角动量间的夹角(恒星倾斜度)的空间和时间演化特征。结果表明,所有样本的最小错位率(20-33%)在统计上一致,且不随轨道距离或系统年龄显著变化,暗示类太阳星的恒星倾斜度主要在形成时期确定,其后续演化有限。这些发现与形成后倾角演化罕见、仅限于小错位或局限于特定参数空间(如有近伴星、高温主星或长周期巨行星的系统)相符。
英文原摘要
The angle between a star's rotational angular momentum vector and the orbital angular momentum vector of its planets---stellar obliquity---encodes the initial conditions and dynamical history of planetary systems. Variations in obliquity with orbital separation may arise from either primordial disk structure (e.g., broken or warped protoplanetary disks with misaligned inner and outer regions) or subsequent dynamical interactions with planets or external perturbers. Comparing obliquities across orbital distance therefore probes the spatial imprint of these processes, while comparisons across age constrain their temporal evolution. To test for signatures of obliquity evolution in both space and time, we compare stellar inclinations with orbital inclinations for 114 single Sun-like stars spanning four samples that probe distinct regimes of orbital separation and age: 49 protoplanetary disk systems, 24 debris disk systems, 21 warm Jupiter systems, and 20 hot Jupiter systems. We find minimum misalignment rates of $33^{+7}_{-6}$% for protoplanetary disk systems, $21^{+9}_{-7}$% for debris disk systems, $23^{+10}_{-8}$% for warm Jupiter systems, and $20^{+9}_{-8}$% for hot Jupiter systems. These rates are consistent within 1$σ$ across all samples. The absence of significant variation in minimum-obliquity distributions with either orbital separation or system age suggests that stellar obliquities for Sun-like stars may be largely established at formation with limited subsequent evolution in both space and time. These results are consistent with post-formation inclination evolution being either infrequent, confined to small misalignments, or restricted to regions of parameter space not examined here---such as systems with close stellar companions, hot host stars, small planets, and long-period giant planets.
hot Jupiter · warm Jupiter

Seven Years of TESS Precovery Observations of the Dwarf-planet Candidate 2026 ED$_{138}$

Te Han, Jeroen Audenaert, Daniel Muthukrishna, Matthew Holman et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用TESS卫星2019-2026年间五个观测周期的七年回顾观测数据，通过TGLC软件移除Gaia恒星和静态残差，沿预测轨道叠加图像以检测矮行星候选体2026 ED138。研究确认了该天体在所有五个观测周期中的可检测性（合并信噪比34σ），TESS位置测量独立验证了JPL轨道解，亮度变化与距离增加和相位角变化预期的0.08等星等衰减相符。这项工作为跨海王星天体的长期轨道监测和精度确定提供了有力支持。
英文原摘要
The trans-Neptunian object 2026 ED$_{138}$ was announced in 2026 October as the first dwarf-planet candidate in our solar system discovered by the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. The Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) observed it in five month-long sectors between 2019 and 2026, four of them before its discovery. We subtract every Gaia DR3 star from these crowded full-frame images with the TESS-Gaia Light Curve (TGLC) package, then remove static residuals with a running median. Stacking the images along its predicted path detects 2026 ED$_{138}$ in all five sectors at 5-21$σ$, and at 34$σ$ combined. The TESS positions alone confirm the JPL orbit, and its brightness is consistent with the 0.08 mag fading expected from its increasing distance and changing phase angle.
transiting exoplanet · planet candidate

An Updated Model for ϵ Eridani b and Prospects for Imaging with the Roman Coronagraph

Jorge Llop-Sayson, Andre Fogal, James Mang, William Thompson et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过贝叶斯框架整合径向速度、绝对位置测量和JWST/NIRCam成像数据,对邻近的类木星系外行星ϵ Eridani b建立了自洽的轨道-大气联合模型。研究确定该行星质量为0.91±0.04木星质量,并发现大气模型对化学平衡假设敏感,清晰大气需要高增强金属丰度而浑浊大气给出更适中的值。通过预测Roman日冕仪的反射光灵敏度,研究表明该仪器有望探测所有浑浊大气模型,而非探测结果将对大气性质形成强约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract ϵ Eridani b, the nearest known Jupiter analog, has its orbit and mass constrained from radial velocity (RV) and absolute astrometry, and its atmosphere from JWST/NIRCam imaging upper limits in A. Sanghi et al. Here we follow up that work with a self-consistent model of ϵ Eridani b that treats all available data within a single Bayesian framework. We extend the RV data with new measurements, update the treatment of the astrometry data with a new model, and include the NIRCam observations with a grid of evolutionary and atmospheric models. We find a mass of 0.91 ± 0.04 M Jup and an orbit consistent with previous work. The imaging data helps constrain planet effective temperature, atmospheric metallicity, and surface gravity. The constraints are dependent on model assumptions: for an atmosphere in chemical equilibrium, an otherwise low statistically significant feature in one of the epochs is recovered as the planet at high confidence. When assuming chemical disequilibrium, the posteriors exhibit a bimodal distribution, with one mode consistent with zero flux and the other coinciding with the flux of the tentative feature. Consistent with previous work, the clear atmosphere models are found to be viable at very enhanced metallicity, whereas the cloudy models yield more moderate values. Applying these models to the Roman Coronagraph, we find that the predicted reflected-light fluxes place every cloudy atmosphere our fit allows within the instrument’s expected sensitivity. A nondetection would be very constraining: a final contrast sensitivity of 2 × 10 −9 would rule out all cloudy atmospheres under our model assumptions.
radial velocity · astrometry
行星大气2 篇

The First Hubble Detection of a Secondary Eclipse from a Rocky Exoplanet: The 5.4 hr Planet TOI-2431 b

Sebastian Zieba, Billy Edwards, Samuel N. Quinn, Leoni Janssen et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用哈勃太空望远镜WFC3对超短周期岩石行星TOI-2431 b进行了相位曲线观测,首次检测到该行星的近红外次级食,结合TESS光学数据约束了其白昼亮度温度、反照率和热重分配特性。观测结果表明该行星亮度温度比接近0.98,表现出日夜间热分布效率低下的特征,但光学和近红外波段热发射与反射光的简并性需要后续JWST观测来消除。这项研究首次利用HST实现了岩石系外行星的次级食检测,为熔融岩行星的大气和物理特性研究建立了重要基准。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present a Hubble Space Telescope (HST) Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) G141 phase-curve observation of the ultrashort-period rocky planet TOI-2431 b and detect its secondary eclipse in the near-infrared. Combined with the TESS optical eclipse, these measurements constrain the planet’s dayside brightness temperature, reflected light contribution, albedo, and heat redistribution. We detect the eclipse in the optical and near-infrared wavelengths of TESS and HST/WFC3 G141, with a planet-to-star flux ratio of 37 ± 10 ppm and 6 9 − 13 + 14 ppm , respectively. Assuming negligible reflected light, the joint TESS+HST fit yields a brightness temperature T b , joint = 252 4 − 84 + 77 K and a brightness temperature ratio of R joint = T b , joint / T p , max = 0.98 0 − 0.034 + 0.032 consistent with the zero-albedo, no-redistribution limit T p , max = 257 4 − 35 + 36 K . The HST phase curve is consistent with zero nightside emission and no measurable hotspot offset, consistent with inefficient day-night heat distribution. Under this thermal-dominated interpretation, TOI-2431 b lies at the zero-albedo, no-redistribution limit and contrasts with previously observed lava worlds. At optical and near-infrared wavelengths, however, thermal emission is degenerate with reflected light. For a geometric albedo of A g = 0.1, reflected light would contribute approximately 11 ppm in each bandpass, reducing the temperature to T b , joint ( A g = 0.1 ) = 240 7 − 102 + 91 K . Longer-wavelength observations are required to isolate the thermal component. Planned JWST/MIRI LRS observations are expected to substantially reduce this degeneracy and provide more direct constraints on heat redistribution and possible silicate-atmosphere spectral features. This marks the first secondary eclipse detection of a rocky exoplanet using HST and establishes TOI-2431 b as a benchmark target for studying properties of lava worlds.
atmosphere

Variable X-Ray Flux May Explain Variable Helium Escape from the Habitable-zone Exoplanet LHS 1140b

Collin Cherubim, Tim Cunningham, Jason A. Dittmann · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究通过XMM-Newton空间望远镜观测宜居带类地行星LHS 1140b周围的X射线和极紫外辐射变化,探究其大气逃逸中氦吸收信号的时间变异性。观测发现X射线通量在18小时内变化幅度为8.5倍,而紫外和可见光通量稳定,长期XUV通量在年际尺度上保持稳定。研究结论认为短期XUV变率和耀斑事件比长期XUV变异更能解释观测到的氦逃逸变异,需要同步进行凌星近红外观测和恒星活动指标监测来确认两者的因果关系。
英文原摘要
Abstract A central goal in exoplanet science is to understand the conditions under which potentially habitable worlds retain atmospheres. This question is of special interest for worlds orbiting M stars, whose habitable-zone planets are more readily observable, but which also emit prolonged high-energy radiation that can erode planetary atmospheres. For one such world, LHS 1140b, a rocky planet orbiting in the habitable zone of an inactive star, time-variable helium absorption has been observed as the planet transited in front of its host star, consistent with atmospheric escape. We present X-ray/ultraviolet flux observations with the XMM-Newton space telescope, which serve to test whether the observed helium absorption variability can be explained by stellar X-ray and extreme-ultraviolet (XUV) flux variability. We detect X-ray flux variability with an amplitude of F max / F min = 8 . 5 − 4.4 + 9.4 within the 18 hr observing window. We do not detect variability in the UV or visible flux. The integrated X-ray flux is fully consistent with the X-ray flux measured in 2018, suggesting stable XUV flux over yearslong timescales. We find that the 2018 observation was not sensitive to the level of variability observed in 2026. We explore several models that fit both datasets and conclude that short-term XUV variability and/or small flares are more likely to explain the helium variability than long-term XUV variability. Simultaneous observations of the planet transit in the near-infrared and stellar activity indicators like XUV flux are critical to confirm the link between the observed helium signal variability and the observed X-ray variability.
atmosphere · atmospheric · atmospheric escape

lenscape: Fast, High-resolution $Δχ^2$ Maps of Microlensing Events

Michael Hippke · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对微引力透镜事件中行星参数简并问题,开发了lenscape软件包用于快速构建Δχ²映射。通过多级邻域传播和自适应轮廓细化策略,该方法显著加速了二元透镜分离-质量比参数空间的搜索,并有效抑制局部极值伪影。在MOA-2022-BLG-091事件中的应用表明,该方法可在台式计算机上于两小时内生成400×400高分辨率映射,为行星微引力透镜事件的参数约束提供了高效工具。
英文原摘要
Planetary microlensing analyses rely on $Δχ^2$ maps across the binary-lens separation $s$ and mass ratio $q$ to locate degenerate solutions and bound parameter uncertainties. Because every grid point requires a full light-curve optimization with finite-source magnifications, constructing these maps is often the primary computational bottleneck in event modeling. Here I present lenscape, an open-source Python package that accelerates $Δχ^2$ mapping and suppresses local-minimum artifacts through multi-stage neighbor propagation and adaptive contour refinement. Applied to the planetary event MOA-2022-BLG-091, lenscape reproduces the published $50\times50$ map in 14 minutes and generates a $400\times400$ map in under two hours on a desktop computer.
microlensing

2026 ED138: A Large Trans-Neptunian Object in Early Rubin Observatory Data, and How Many More May Remain

Matthew J. Holman, Mario Jurić, Kevin J. Napier, Jacob A. Kurlander et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用Rubin天文台数据发现并表征了大型TNO天体2026 ED138，其绝对星等为3.19（直径612-1530 km），是已知最亮的十大TNO之一，且为矮行星候选体，其轨道位于海王星12:7平均运动共振的边缘。通过对已知亮TNO的回收统计，我们估计银河面外还有约3个及更多类似天体未被发现，而银河面内10°范围内可能存在更多未被现有巡天覆盖的大型TNO。
英文原摘要
We characterize a large trans-Neptunian object (TNO), 2026 ED138, discovered in data taken by the NSF-DOE Vera C. Rubin Observatory with the linking code pointdexter, and validate it independently. 2026 ED138 has absolute magnitude $H_V=3.19$, implying a diameter of 612-1530 km for geometric albedos between 0.25 and 0.04 and making it one of the ten intrinsically brightest known TNOs and a dwarf-planet candidate. At $i\approx19.4$ it is bright enough in the sky for ground-based spectroscopy and light curves and for detailed study with HST and JWST. Archival DECam detections extend its arc to more than ten years (3789 days). Its orbit, with barycentric $a=43.09$ au, $e=0.139$ and $i=24.4^\circ$, lies at the edge of the 12:7 mean-motion resonance with Neptune, like that of the dwarf planet Haumea, but with $g-r=0.63$ it is too red to be a member of Haumea's collisional family. 2026 ED138 was missed by earlier surveys because it lies far south and near the crowded regions of the Galactic plane. Our recoveries of the known TNOs with $H_V<4$ suggest that about 3 more objects like them, and probably more, remain undiscovered away from the Galactic plane. More such objects probably lie within $10^\circ$ of the plane, which no search has yet covered to this depth.
planet candidate

PyMsOfa 2.0: A Pure-Python Implementation of the IAU Standards of Fundamental Astronomy Service

Dongjie Tan, Jianghui Ji, Jiacheng Liu · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
PyMsOfa 2.0 使用NumPy将国际天文学联合会(IAU)基础天文学标准(SOFA)库的全部247个例程重新实现为纯Python代码，无需编译外部库即可在多平台运行，并支持批量处理NumPy数组以便用于巡天管道和蒙特卡洛模拟。该实现保持与官方SOFA C版本的算法一致性，经过逐例程验证后达到机器精度或文档规定的精度要求，已应用于盖亚星表天体测量归约和CHES任务的天体测量研究。该软件包还包含改进的章动模型IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$等扩展模块，通过PyPI、GitHub和Zenodo发布。
英文原摘要
Astrometric calculations require authoritative algorithms so that every result is correct and reproducible. The Standards of Fundamental Astronomy (SOFA) service of the International Astronomical Union (IAU) provides such algorithms, officially released only in Fortran 77 and ANSI C. In an earlier paper we presented the first version of PyMsOfa, a Python package wrapping the SOFA C library. Here we present PyMsOfa 2.0, which re-implements the complete SOFA library in pure Python with NumPy. All 247 official SOFA routines are ported directly to Python, requiring no compilation of any external C library and running identically on Linux, Windows, and macOS. The routines operate on NumPy arrays, so arguments can be processed in batch, which is convenient for survey pipelines and Monte Carlo simulations. The package also provides an extension module with validated models not yet part of the official SOFA release, such as the improved precession--nutation model IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$. The reimplementation reproduces the official SOFA algorithms: all constants, series, and coefficients are taken unchanged from the SOFA source files, and routine names differ only by the pym prefix. We verify every routine against the official SOFA C release: most reproduce the C results to machine precision, the remainder within the accuracy tolerance stated in the SOFA documentation. The package has been applied to Gaia astrometric reduction and used in astrometric studies of the Closeby Habitable Exoplanet Survey (CHES) mission, and is available through PyPI, GitHub, and Zenodo.
astrometric

Multiple Regular Frequency Spacings in δ Scuti Stars from Automated Analysis of TESS Oscillation Spectra

Shravan M. Hanasoge, Shashwat Chamoli, Subrata Kumar Panda, Daniel R. Reese · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究针对快速自转δ Scuti星的振荡频谱难以解释的问题,开发了一套自动化统计框架来识别近似等间距的模式序列,并应用于TESS观测的4681颗δ Scuti星的振荡谱。分析结果表明许多星体内存在多个不同的等间距频率序列,这一现象超出了现有理论对快速自转声学模式的预测,暗示可能存在未知的模式族或其他物理机制控制这些星体的振荡谱结构。该方法为大规模恒星巡测中的规则模式序列研究提供了可扩展的工具和新的观测约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract The oscillation spectra of rapidly rotating δ Scuti stars are difficult to interpret because rotation modifies both the frequencies and spatial structure of the modes. Although theoretical studies predict that acoustic modes should organize into approximately equally spaced frequency sequences, systematic searches for such patterns have been limited by the absence of scalable, statistically robust detection methods. We present an automated statistical framework for identifying approximately equally spaced mode sequences in stellar oscillation spectra while quantifying the significance of each candidate detection. We apply this method to oscillation spectra of 4681 δ Scuti stars observed by the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). The analysis identifies regular mode sequences in a large number of stars and, in many cases, reveals multiple independent regular frequency spacings within the same oscillation spectrum. While the existence of regular sequences is broadly consistent with theoretical expectations for rapidly rotating acoustic modes, the occurrence of multiple dissimilar spacings is not readily explained by current models. Differences in acoustic travel times between known island-mode families appear insufficient to account for the observed diversity of spacings, suggesting that additional mode families or other physical effects contribute to the organization of oscillation spectra of δ Scuti stars. Our results provide a scalable framework for studying regular mode sequences in large stellar surveys and new observational constraints on oscillations in rapidly rotating stars.
transiting exoplanet

Discovery and Characterization of the TOI-4468 Planetary System: A Transiting Hot Jupiter with a Lone Nearby Outer Companion

Joseph R. Livesey, Benjamin J. Hord, Juliette Becker, Andrew Vanderburg et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过联合NEID径向速度测量和TESS及地面观测的凌星光度测量，发现并表征了TOI-4468系统中的热木星TOI-4468 b和近邻亚海王星TOI-4468 c，该系统独特之处在于热木星外侧伴星的孤立配置而无内侧伴星。观测结果显示该系统未经历过激发相互倾角的早期世俗共振，这对理解热木星与外伴星的几何构型及热木星形成机制提供了重要约束。研究还识别出可能存在的624天周期外侧巨行星信号，为该系统的动力学演化和形成历史提供了新的研究线索。
英文原摘要
Abstract We report the discovery of two planets, a hot Jupiter (HJ) and a nearby outer sub-Neptune, orbiting the star TOI-4468. This system is unique among the current exoplanet census in that it features a close outer companion to a HJ without an accompanying inner companion. By jointly fitting radial velocity (RV) measurements taken with the NEID spectrograph and transit photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) and several ground-based observatories, we constrain the orbital periods, masses, and radii of these two planets. We confirm the planetary nature of the HJ TOI-4468 b ( R = 1.01 R J , m = 0.54 M J , P = 2.77 days). We also validate the outer planet TOI-4468 c ( R = 0.28 R J , P = 7.01 days) statistically, incorporating constraints from ground-based observations. We also identify, but cannot confirm, an additional RV signal that may be due to an outer giant in this system with an orbital period of 624 days. From the observed geometry of this system, we argue that it must never have encountered an early secular resonance that is thought to excite the mutual inclination of other HJ/outer companion systems. We discuss the possibility of an undetected inner companion, as well as potential implications for HJ formation.
radial velocity · transiting exoplanet

Asteroseismic properties of young pulsators in the Perseus molecular cloud. Discovery of period-spacing patterns in HD 281229

Saakshi Wadhwa, Dario J. Fritzewski, Gang Li, Simon J. Murphy · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用TESS测光数据在英仙座分子云中识别和表征年轻脉动星,特别是中等质量的前主序星,以探测其内部结构和早期演化阶段的旋转特性。通过Lomb-Scargle周期图分析和迭代预白化方法,我们发现8个压力模式脉动星和2个重力模式候选体,其中HD 281229是迄今发现最年轻的具有可检测周期间距图样的恒星。通过同时拟合偶极和四极周期间距图样,我们获得了该星的渐近周期间距和近核旋转频率,证明了重力模式星震学在探测极早期演化阶段恒星内部结构和旋转性质方面的有效性。
英文原摘要
Very young pulsating stars, including pre-main sequence pulsators, provide a rare opportunity to probe stellar interiors and investigate rotation during the earliest phases of stellar evolution. However, they remain relatively unexplored, particularly in the intermediate-mass regime. We aim to identify and characterise young pulsating stars in the Perseus molecular cloud using photometric data from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). We compiled -based membership catalogues for the Perseus molecular cloud, applied selection criteria to isolate young intermediate-mass stars, and extracted light curves from TESS full-frame images. Variability classification was performed using Lomb--Scargle periodograms and iterative pre-whitening. For confirmed pressure-mode pulsators, we estimated the frequency of maximum pulsation power, ν_ Gaia max . For gravity-mode candidates, we searched for period-spacing patterns and fitted them using the traditional approximation of rotation. From the 148 membership-selected targets, we identified eight pressure-mode pulsators and two objects showing possible gravity-mode pulsations. We also analysed the previously known δ Sct star V705 Per, bringing the total sample to 149 targets. One of the gravity-mode pulsators, HD 281229, is the youngest star known to date with a detected and modelled period-spacing pattern. By simultaneously fitting the dipole and quadrupole period-spacing patterns, we estimate an asymptotic period spacing of (Π_0=4776 ) and a near-core rotation frequency of (f_ =1.317 ^ s rot d -1 ). These results highlight the potential of asteroseismology to probe the internal structure and rotation of very young stars. The detected period-spacing pattern in HD 281229 shows that gravity-mode asteroseismology can provide meaningful constraints on the properties of stars at early evolutionary stages.

行星形成与盘演化2 篇

The colors of protoplanetary disks. Impact of nonspherical dust particles on the polarized and total intensity colors

M. Roumesy, J. Ma, R. Tazaki, F. Ménard et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过三维辐射传输模型生成原行星盘的多波长散射光合成图像，系统研究了非球形尘埃颗粒的大小、形态和成分对盘色（偏振和总强度）的影响。研究发现偏振色对尘埃性质的敏感性更高，聚集体的单体尺寸而非聚集体尺寸是决定颜色的关键因素，且有效介质理论仅对低孔隙度聚集体适用。将色度测量与散射相函数结合可有效打破尘埃模型的简并性，对HD 100546的应用表明其红外色排除了致密颗粒和富有机物成分，而与含大单体的无定形碳聚集体相符。
英文原摘要
Dust properties in protoplanetary disks remain poorly understood despite their central role in planetary formation. A detailed observational characterization of their properties is crucial to improving our understanding of grain growth. Recent studies, based on analyses of scattered-light images and scattering phase functions, provide promising insights into dust characterization. However, the phase function alone is insufficient to constrain dust properties efficiently. In particular, degeneracies between dust size, composition, and morphology remain a major obstacle to linking observations to realistic dust models. We investigate the behavior of the disk color as a function of dust size, shape, and composition. The goal is to combine this with phase function measurements to most effectively constrain the dust properties. We used the 3D radiative transfer code MCFOST to generate synthetic scattered-light images of protoplanetary disks from the visible to the near-infrared, in both total and polarized intensity. We considered compact spherical grains using Mie theory. We used the optical properties computed with the T-matrix method or the discrete dipole approximation for irregular particles and for compact and fractal aggregates from the AggScatVIR database. We measured the disk colors directly from the synthetic images. We obtain five main results. (i) For aggregates larger than the wavelength, the monomer size plays a crucial role in determining the colors, whereas the aggregate size has a negligible effect, especially for low fractal dimensions. (ii) The polarized color is more sensitive to the dust properties than the total intensity color, likely because it combines the wavelength dependences of both the scattered intensity and the degree of polarization. (iii) The effective medium theory is a reliable approximation only for relatively compact aggregates composed of small monomers and becomes inaccurate for highly porous aggregates or those composed of larger monomers. (iv) The effective albedo is useful for predicting disk colors, but radiative transfer calculations are still required to account for the multiple scattering effect and quantitatively interpret disk colors. (v) For HD 100546, the red optical to near-infrared colors exclude compact grains and organic-rich compositions but are compatible with fractal or compact aggregates of amorphous carbon composition containing large monomers (200--400 nm). Our study of color based on synthetic data and its application to the example of the disk around HD 100546 shows that combining color measurements, or a color measurement with a polarized scattering phase function, offers a promising approach to breaking the degeneracy between dust models. Extending this approach to larger samples of disks with multiwavelength observations should provide better constraints on the properties and evolution of dust and motivate the use of color in future studies of protoplanetary disks.
protoplanetary disk

Diversity of planetary origins and compositions traced by stellar abundance differences among co-natal star systems

Bertram Bitsch, Fan Liu, Haiyang S. Wang · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过分析共生恒星对中的恒星丰度差异，推断曾被恒星吸积的行星的组成成分。研究发现，难熔元素（Fe、Mg、Si）和挥发性元素（O、C、S）丰度差异的不同组合模式反映了行星在不同位置（相对于水和碳晶粒蒸发线）的形成历史，以及行星在FeS蒸发线处迁移的证据。这些结果表明行星的不同组成与其形成位置直接相关，为理解系外行星组成和行星移动过程提供了新约束。
英文原摘要
So called co-natal stars are wide binaries that form from the same molecular cloud and are expected to share the same stellar composition. However, observations indicate stellar abundance differences in a small fraction of these co-natal systems. These stellar abundance differences have been attributed to the ingestion of planets by their host stars. In this study, we analyze a previous sample of co-natal stars to determine the composition of the planets that had been ingested by their host stars. The abundance differences in these co-natal pairs come in different flavours, where systems can show a positive abundance difference of the refractory elements (Fe, Mg, Si) paired with a positive, zero, or negative abundance difference of volatiles (O, C, S). Positive trends of refractory and volatile abundance differences can be explained (at minimum) by the ingestion of a single planet by one of the stars. However, positive trends of refractory abundance differences paired with zero or negative abundance differences of oxygen and carbon imply that both stars are required to ingest planets formed at different locations (i.e. interior or exterior to the water and/or carbon grain evaporation front). Furthermore, some systems show a sulfur abundance difference that is a factor of a few lower than the abundance difference of Fe, Si, and Mg, even though sulfur is mostly accreted via the moderately volatile compound FeS. We show that this can be explained by the migration of one of the planets across the FeS evaporation front, where the planet first accretes large amounts of sulfur with the solids and then no sulfur, while still accreting the other refractory elements before being ingested by its host star. Our study shows that the planetary compositions derived from the detailed stellar abundances are sufficient to explain the measured abundance differences of co-natal stars, giving us a handle on the composition of exoplanets. At the same time it implies very different planetary compositions caused by the different formation location of these planets, in agreement with planet formation models dedicated to the formation of inner super-Earths and sub-Neptunes.
planet formation

Coupled evolution of Earth's H2-rich proto-atmosphere and oxidized magma ocean: Efficient H2O production through dissolved H2 transport and atmospheric buffering of the magma ocean redox state

Tatsuya Yoshida, Kirara Arima, Naoki Terada, Takeshi Kuroda et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究建立了耦合大气-地幔演化模型，包含原始地球岩浆洋与H2富集原始大气之间的扩散输运、熔体内氧化还原反应及H2O生成过程，模拟了行星冷却过程中两个圈层的化学耦合。结果表明，岩浆洋表面冷却驱动的密集对流使边界层快速更新，促进了大气H2向岩浆洋的输运和H2O高效生成，使两个圈层能在约10^4年内化学平衡。这一耦合演化过程通过大气与岩浆洋的氧化还原交换，既可缓冲固结过程中的极端氧化，同时有助于维持还原性H2富集大气，对早期地球水库的形成具有重要贡献。
英文原摘要
Giant impacts during Earth's final accretion likely generated global magma oceans, where the disproportionation of Fe^2+ at lower-mantle pressures and segregation of the resulting metallic iron into the core could have oxidized the magma ocean. Earth may nevertheless have had an H2-rich proto-atmosphere in chemical disequilibrium with the magma ocean, but the chemical coupling between this atmosphere and an oxidized magma ocean remains poorly constrained. Here we develop a coupled atmosphere-interior evolution model that includes diffusive transport in the boundary layer between the magma ocean and atmosphere, redox reactions in the melt, partitioning of oxide components and H2O among coexisting phases, and planetary cooling through a proto-atmosphere, with metallic iron produced by Fe^2+ disproportionation removed into the core as it forms. Atmospheric H2 transport into the magma ocean and subsequent H2O production become more efficient with increasingly frequent boundary layer renewal driven by buoyant overturn of dense melt cooled at the surface, allowing the atmosphere and magma ocean to chemically equilibrate within ~10^4 yr when the magma ocean is sufficiently deep and hot to extend into the lower mantle. Redox exchange with the atmosphere buffers the solidification-driven redox evolution of the magma ocean, preventing extreme oxidation or reduction of the coupled system. These results suggest that the coupled evolution could have contributed to early Earth's water inventory while maintaining a reducing H2-rich atmosphere.

The 3D Cosmic Shoreline for Nurturing Planetary Atmospheres

Zach K. Berta-Thompson, Patcharapol Wachiraphan, Catriona A. Murray · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究建立了一个统计框架，在逃逸速度、行星接收的恒星辐射通量和恒星光度构成的三维参数空间中，推导行星大气存在的宇宙分界线位置、形态和不确定性。通过将恒星光度显式纳入模型以避免难以测量的历史X射线和极端紫外辐射通量，研究发现临界通量阈值与逃逸速度的幂律关系指数为p=5.90±0.52（陡于经典值4），与恒星光度的关系指数为q=1.17±0.24，这意味着在光度低于log₁₀(L⋆/L⊙)=-2.23的M矮星（约M4V型）外，地球大小行星即使位于宜居带也难以保留大气。该模型为预测任意行星保有大气的概率提供了定量工具，可被近期JWST岩石行星观测严格检验。
英文原摘要
Abstract Various “cosmic shorelines” have been proposed to delineate which planets have atmospheres. The fates of individual planetary atmospheres may be set by a complex sea of growth and loss processes, driven by unmeasurable environmental factors or unknown historical events. Yet, defining population-level boundaries helps illuminate which processes matter and identify high-priority targets for future atmospheric searches. Here, we provide a statistical framework for inferring the position, shape, and fuzziness of an instellation-based cosmic shoreline, defined in the 3D space of planet escape velocity, planet bolometric flux received, and host-star luminosity. We circumvent the need to estimate each individual host star’s historical X-ray and extreme ultraviolet fluences by including luminosity in the definition of the shoreline, explicitly modeling how sharply such drivers of atmospheric escape intensify toward lower-luminosity M dwarf stars and marginalizing over the associated uncertainties. Using solar system and exoplanet atmospheric constraints, under the assumption that one planar boundary applies across a wide parameter space, we find the critical flux threshold for atmospheres scales with escape velocity with a power-law index of p = 5 . 90 − 0.43 + 0.61 , steeper than the canonical literature slope of p = 4, and scales with stellar luminosity with a power-law index of q = 1.1 7 − 0.20 + 0.28 , steep enough to disfavor atmospheres on Earth-sized planets out to the habitable zone for stars less luminous than log 10 ( L ⋆ / L ⊙ ) = − 2.2 3 − 0.21 + 0.18 (roughly spectral type M4V). This model provides quantitative predictions for the probability that any given planet possesses an atmosphere, which will be rigorously tested by upcoming JWST Rocky Worlds observations.

Deep H α Imaging Survey of IC 348 with the Hubble Space Telescope. I. Accretion Properties of Stellar and Substellar Objects

Lillian Yushu Jiang, Brendan P. Bowler, Yifan Zhou, Adam L. Kraus et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究基于哈勃空间望远镜的深度Hα成像观测，对IC 348星团（~2 Myr）中200个成员天体（跨越3 M⊙至4 MJup的恒星-亚恒星质量范围）进行了均匀的吸积率分析。结果显示37%的样本中检测到吸积，且纳入弱吸积者和非探测上限后，质量吸积率-恒星质量关系的斜率下降且散度增大，特别是对于自由浮动的行星质量天体，其吸积率被高估约一个数量级。这项研究表明样本选择（是否限制于有盘或吸积天体）对种群级吸积关系的形状具有主导影响。
英文原摘要
Abstract Accretion governs the growth of young stars and the early evolution of their circumstellar disks, yet population-level measurements of accretion are often hampered by heterogeneous diagnostics and by samples preferentially selected toward disk-bearing or accreting objects. This can impact the mass accretion rate–stellar mass ( M ̇ – M ⋆ ) relation, particularly at substellar masses. We present a uniform analysis of accretion in the ∼2 Myr old star-forming cluster IC 348 based on deep Hubble Space Telescope (HST) F656N imaging. Using H α excess as a single, homogeneous accretion tracer, we derive accretion rates and robust upper limits for 200 cluster members spanning the stellar-to-substellar mass regime (3 M ⊙ to 4 M Jup ). Accretion is detected in 37% ± 3% of the sample, with fractions of 34% ± 4% among stellar members and 46% ± 6% among substellar objects. For accretors alone, the inferred M ̇ – M ⋆ relation is consistent with those measured in similarly aged regions such as Lupus. In contrast, including weak accretors and nondetections increases the scatter and flattens the slope while lowering the intercept, demonstrating the strong influence of the low-accretion tail on population-level accretion relations. For free-floating planetary-mass objects in IC 348, extrapolating the accretors-only fit overpredicts M ̇ by approximately an order of magnitude compared to the fit that includes upper limits, which instead implies mass accretion rates of <10 −12 M ⊙ yr −1 . These results show that sample selection—specifically whether the sample is restricted to disk-bearing or accreting targets or instead is drawn from a complete membership census—is a dominant factor shaping population-level accretion relations across a wide dynamic range in stellar mass.

Space-based astrometric missions such as and rely on one-dimensional scanning strategies that couple the measured signals with both observation times and scanning geometry. This coupling can introduce spurious periodicities and complicate their interpretation. We present a non-parametric extension of the phase-distance correlation (PDC) periodogram that quantifies and reduces dependence on scan geometry at the period-search stage. Using data, we identified recurrent periodicities associated with characteristic scanning-law timescales and showed that they are strongly suppressed by the scan-angle-controlled statistic. We then applied the method to Data Release 4 (DR4) prerelease epoch astrometry, where a recurrent ∼54-day feature is suppressed across multiple sources while independently known orbital signals remain detectable. The method provides a diagnostic of scan-angle dependence and a way to mitigate its impact on period searches. These results are particularly relevant for DR4, where public epoch astrometry will enable large-scale searches for companions ranging from exoplanets to dormant black holes. We present JWST/MIRI Low Resolution Spectroscopy (LRS) of 3I/ATLAS obtained post-perihelion at a heliocentric distance of 2.21 au. Our spectrum contains a prominent 10 micron silicate emission feature with a broad, smooth shape that is distinct from those commonly observed in Solar System comets. We model the spectrum using thermal emission from amorphous carbon, amorphous olivine, and amorphous pyroxene grains. Our models strongly favor a pyroxene-dominated, amorphous silicate composition and show a degeneracy between silicate grain size and the relative abundance of silicate and carbon dust. We identify an emission feature near 6.83 microns that is consistent with aliphatic C-H-bearing material. V1298,Tau is a very young and magnetically active K1V star that hosts a benchmark multi-planetary system in which planet formation and evolutionary history can be studied at the earliest stages. Because of the high interest in this system, it has been the target of a number of multi-wavelength follow-up so far. We also investigated multi-band correlations to link the emission to stellar activity. Radio observations were carried out with the Karl G. Spatial variability of CH4-to-CO conversion pathways in irradiated gas giant atmospheres: implications for chemical relaxation and 1D quench schemes

Maria Zamyatina, Ãric Hébrard, Harry Baskett, Roméo Veillet et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过三维全球环流模型模拟具有热化学反应的系外气态巨行星大气，揭示了CH₄到CO的转化途径随深度、经度和行星而变化的空间结构，发现1D假设将大气分层设置为垂直均匀的方法在描述化学非平衡时存在显著偏差。研究表明虽然1D淬灭方案能在较好的精度范围内定位淬灭层，但在淬灭层上方完全失效，且对次要碳承体的丰度预测误差可达两个数量级，这对精确模拟系外行星大气组成具有重要启示。
英文原摘要
Abstract Close-in gas giant atmospheres are in chemical disequilibrium. Solving 3D kinetics to capture one source of this disequilibrium, transport-induced quenching, is computationally demanding, so simplifications are used. General circulation models (GCMs) relax abundances on the timescale of a single conversion pathway. 1D models place a quench level and hold abundances vertically uniform above it. We test both simplifications on CH4 against aerosol-free, solar-composition GCM simulations with thermochemical kinetics for HAT-P-11b, HD 189733b, HD 209458b and WASP-17b. We find ten CH4-to-CO conversion pathways, varying with depth, longitude and planet. A single pathway controls CH4 quenching on the three cooler planets; on WASP-17b two compete. The CH4 quench level is 3D: where the atmosphere is hotter, or a faster pathway takes over, the conversion timescale shortens, the quench level rises and the quench-level abundance falls. On WASP-17b the pathway switch lifts the quench level over the substellar tropics, deepening equatorial CH4 depletion. A 1D quench treatment with the Zahnle & Marley timescale typically locates the quench level within a factor of two in pressure for CH4 and about four for CO on every planet. It fails above the quench level, where every quench scheme holds abundances vertically uniform. The minor carbon carrier, CH4 or CO depending on the planet, is wrong by up to two orders of magnitude, on HD 189733b in opposite directions at different latitudes. Quenching sets an abundance at the quench level but does not fix it thereafter; even without photochemistry, transport and conversion keep that abundance spatially structured.

Abstract The advent of large-scale time-domain surveys provides both opportunities and challenges for understanding accretion disk evolution in cataclysmic variables (CVs). Using high-cadence photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS), we investigate the eclipsing SU UMa-type dwarf nova Z Cha. Leveraging eclipses as a natural probe, we examine the evolution of the accretion disk through variations in eclipse depth, O – C of eclipse minima, and positive superhump (PSH) amplitude. During superoutbursts, all three quantities exhibit quasiperiodic modulations with a common period of ∼2 days, consistent with the precession period of an eccentric disk. We interpret these correlated variations as evidence of an eccentric, precessing disk: O – C traces the periodic shift of the system’s brightness center, while eclipse depth and PSH amplitude vary with the orientation of the disk bulge relative to the line of sight. In quiescence (Sectors 13 and 93), PSHs with periods of ∼0.0762 days show linearly decreasing amplitudes and periods, indicating gradual shrinkage of the eccentric disk and a slowing precession. Remarkably, a coherent signal with a period of ∼0.0729 days ( ϵ − ≈ −0.02) appears in the same quiescent intervals. This signal may represent negative superhumps (NSHs) coexisting with PSHs, although an orbital sideband of the PSH cannot presently be excluded with the available data. If confirmed as NSHs, their coexistence with PSHs would challenge the classical tilted-disk model, and could be explained by retrograde apsidal precession of an eccentric disk, where the inner disk precesses retrogradely (NSHs) and the outer disk progradely (PSHs); this interpretation remains to be tested by further observations.

英文原摘要
Abstract Understanding the evolution of solids in protoplanetary discs is important to comprehend the formation of giant planets cores and terrestrial planets. The ALMA Large Program AGE-PRO studied key disc properties in a survey of 30 protoplanetary discs across three evolutionary stages: the embedded phase (0.5-1 Myr), the middle-age phase (1-3 Myr), and the end of the disc lifetime (2-6 Myr). Preliminary dust properties were estimated using 1.3 mm observations, but a multi-wavelength analysis provides a better constraint of important properties such as the dust disc mass, the dust temperature, and the maximum grain size. We present new ALMA 3.1 mm and 1.5 mm (Bands 3 and 5) continuum observations of the complete survey of 30 AGE-PRO discs. We fitted the observed visibilities and derived radial brightness profiles, spatially resolving 20 discs from the AGE-PRO sample at the two studied wavelengths. A multi-wavelength modelling was performed including also the radial profiles from the 1.3 mm observations. Our results replicate trends obtained from the single wavelength analysis: the dust disc size seems to be constant over age and the dust disc mass decreases with time, with a factor ∼10-40 uncertainty from opacity assumptions. This result implies much lower gas-to-dust mass ratios for the discs in our sample than previously reported. In addition, the derived spectral indices are consistent with optically thick emission in a large fraction of the AGE-PRO discs, in particular for the Ophiuchus and Lupus discs.

Contribution of White Dwarf Formation Kicks to the Free-floating Planet Population

Alexander P. Stephan, Keivan G. Stassun · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究探讨了白矮星形成过程中的反冲速度作为自由浮动行星(FFPs)的一个重要形成机制。通过动力学模拟,我们发现虽然白矮星反冲直接解除仅约1%已知行星系统的束缚,但会在≳40%的长周期多行星系统中引发动力学不稳定性,导致大多数白矮星周围的行星被抛出形成FFPs。由于白矮星反冲速度较低,这些被抛出的FFPs在其前主星增强的AGB阶段获得加热,可在形成后数百万年内与前主星保持关联,构成一个具有独特观测特征的FFP亚群体,约占银河系FFPs的数个百分比。
英文原摘要
Abstract Free-floating planets (FFPs) are a distinct class of exoplanets that do not orbit stars but are nevertheless found to be very common. A variety of formation mechanisms have been proposed as their origin, such as “starlike” direct collapse from gas and dust clouds or ejection from young planetary systems via dynamical instabilities. Here, another possible formation scenario is explored that would instead apply for old planetary systems, in the form of white dwarf (WD) formation kicks. Observations over recent years have shown that WDs experience a mild recoil kick during their formation from asymptotic giant branch (AGB) stars. Here we show that, while WD formation kicks directly unbind only ∼1% of the known planet and exoplanet population, they drive dynamical instabilities in ≳40% of known long-period multiplanet systems, likely leading to planet ejection and FFP generation by most WDs. We also show that FFPs generated via WD kicks will have undergone potentially significant and long-lasting heating via their host stars’ enhanced AGB luminosities. Given the low ejection velocities due to the weakness of the WD kicks, such warmed FFPs can thus be associated with their former host stars for several Myr after formation. Therefore, WD formation kicks contribute a distinct, observationally identifiable FFP subpopulation comprising a few percent of the Galaxy’s FFPs, relevant for the design of future FFP surveys.
free-floating planet · planet ejection

The anomalously large transiting planet around Boyajian's star: evidence for a circumplanetary ring

Wei Zhu, Quanyi Liu · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对博亚吉安星周围巨行星候选体半径异常偏大的问题，通过开发快速精确的环行星凌星光变曲线计算代码，对TESS观测数据进行拟合分析。研究表明该行星实际上被一个不透明、高度扁平的环系统所环绕，环的外半径延伸至行星半径的3.1-4.4倍，具有近极向的倾角。该环模型消除了行星的半径异常，且与该星历史性昏暗事件推断出的尘埃-彗星牧羊环境在物理图像上相一致。
英文原摘要
A recent study reported a transiting giant-planet candidate with a mass of $9.4^{+4.9}_{-4.1}\,M_{\rm J}$ and a radius of $1.7\pm0.1\,R_{\rm J}$ on a wide orbit around Boyajian's star (KIC 8462852, also called Tabby's star), which is well known for its irregular, deep flux dips. The reported radius is puzzling, because cool giant planets of $\sim$10\,$M_{\rm Jup}$ are expected to have radii close to Jupiter's, so the companion appears $\sim$60--70\% larger than standard models allow. Here we explore the solution that the planet is actually ringed. We develop a fast and accurate code to compute the transit light curve due to a ringed planet, and apply it to the TESS light curve. The ring model is marginally favoured by the data by $Δχ^2>10$ and a difference in Bayesian information criterion (BIC) of $<2$. The preferred configuration is a Jupiter-sized ($1.1\,R_{\rm J}$) planet surrounded by an opaque, highly flattened ring extending to $3.1$--$4.4$ planetary radii with a nearly polar obliquity. While the ring geometry is uncertain, simple estimates suggest that a debris annulus extending out to the planet's Roche limit is needed to explain the large transit depth. The ring thus removes the radius anomaly altogether and is qualitatively consistent with the dusty, comet-shepherding environment inferred from the star's historic dimmings.

Frequent X-class Far-ultraviolet Flares from the Young Solar Analog DS Tucanae A

Tristen M. Sextro, Kevin France, Vladimir S. Airapetian, Grace M. Sweetak et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过哈勃空间望远镜对年轻太阳类星DS Tuc A进行约11小时的远紫外波段观测，发现该星在观测期间产生26次X级耀斑，耀斑能量达10²⁹-10³¹尔格，持续时间为90-960秒。研究详细分析了各耀斑的能谱特性、电子密度和X射线亮度等性质，为理解早期太阳对行星磁层和大气环境的影响提供了关键约束，同时为研究耀斑对绕行星DS Tuc A b的影响奠定了观测基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract The young solar analog DS Tuc A (∼45 million yr old, spectral type G6V) serves as an excellent proxy to the Sun during early stages of the Earth’s evolution. The young Sun’s enhanced magnetic activity in the form of X-ray and extreme UV emission, stellar wind, and flares likely affected the magnetospheric and atmospheric environments of early Venus, Earth, and Mars. Studies of DS Tuc A help constrain the evolution of the magnetospheric and atmospheric environments of early Venus, Earth, and Mars and create opportunities to study the impact of flares on the transiting exoplanet, DS Tuc A b. We analyze ∼11 hr of far-ultraviolet (FUV; 1130–1450 Å) observations taken by the Hubble Space Telescope Cosmic Origins Spectrograph during late 2023 and early 2024. We find that DS Tuc A exhibited 26 flares over ∼11.1 hr of observations in the transition region lines Si IV (1394 Å + 1402 Å), with durations ranging from 90 to 960 s and FUV absolute energies ranging from ∼10 29 to 10 31 erg over the observed bandpass. Si IV flares are accompanied by increases of other chromospheric and transition region lines; however, the strongest Si IV flares show no enhanced coronal emission traced by Fe XXI 1354 Å. We present an analysis of each detected flare, including X-ray and bolometric luminosities scaled from FUV flare energies, electron density measurements for the strongest flares, and the FUV continuum response to flaring. These results provide constraints on the frequency and energetics of flares comparable to X100-class solar flares and FUV-emitting regions in young solar analogs.

You Shall Not Pass (Without Modeling): High-resolution Analysis of KMT-2019-BLG-0253 using MORIA

T. Dex Bhadra, Sean K. Terry, David P. Bennett, Aparna Bhattacharya et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究开发了MORIA管道，用于自动化处理哈勃太空望远镜高分辨率微引力透镜观测数据，通过多星点扩散函数拟合解决严重混光问题。应用于微引力透镜事件KMT-2019-BLG-0253，确定了主星质量(0.65±0.04 M☉)、行星质量(7.18±0.40 M⊕或9.48±1.13 M⊕)和透镜距离(2.64±0.22 kpc)，将可行解减少50%。该工作强调了高分辨率成像分析在罗曼太空望远镜银河系凸起巡天中微引力透镜行星研究中的关键作用。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present the Microlensing Object high-Resolution Imaging Analysis pipeline, or MORIA . This is an automated procedure to reduce high-resolution Hubble Space Telescope (HST) images of microlensing targets, build empirical point-spread function (PSF) models from the data, and perform simultaneous multistar PSF fitting to blended sources, lenses, and neighbor stars. We have developed and tested this pipeline using HST observations of the microlensing event KMT-2019-BLG-0253, which shows clear evidence of three highly blended stars in HST. Under our assumed source and lens identification, we determine a host mass of M host = 0.65 ± 0.04 M ⊙ . We have reduced the number of possible solutions for this target by a factor of 2, with the remaining solution subject to the well-known close/wide degeneracy. We determine a planet mass of m p = 7.18 ± 0.40 M ⊕ (close) or m p = 9.48 ± 1.13 M ⊕ (wide), and distance to the lens system of D L = 2.64 ± 0.22 kpc. This target lies within the upcoming Roman Telescope Galactic Bulge Time Domain Survey (GBTDS) field. Future analysis of the Roman data for this event can confirm or reject the initial source and lens identifications presented in this work. Finally, this work demonstrates the importance of using an automated high-resolution imaging tool to inform lightcurve modeling for microlensing planets found during the GBTDS.

UV-driven organic precursor formation and dust evolution outside the snow line

Ivan Judah Manoraj, Satoshi Okuzumi, Kazuaki Homma · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过垂直尘埃演化模拟和光化学计算,考察原行星盘雪线外紫外辐照驱动的有机前驱体形成及其对尘埃演化的影响。研究发现恒星莱曼α辐射通过共振散射能够显著增加光产物丰度,足以在尘粒表面形成约10%的有机-岩石比外壳。这些紫外驱动的有机合成可以增强尘粒粘着性,促进尘埃生长,从而有助于克服类地行星形成的屏障。
英文原摘要
In the outer regions of protoplanetary disks, dust grains are often mantled with a layer of ice containing simple molecules. The irradiation of the disk by far-ultraviolet (FUV) photons breaks such molecules, producing photoproducts (radicals, ions, and intermediary molecules) in the ice layer that can act as precursors to complex organics. As grains drift towards the warmer, inner region of the disk, their consequent sublimation of ices causes organic synthesis on their surfaces, potentially enhancing grain sticking and dust growth. In addition to interstellar medium radiation, FUV emission from the central star and nearby massive stars can contribute to photoproduct formation. We perform a vertical dust size evolution simulation including grain growth and fragmentation, and calculate photoproduct formation under irradiation from these three sources, treating the central star's FUV continuum and Lyman-$α$ emission separately. We find that enhanced UV irradiation, particularly from stellar Lyman-$α$ emission, significantly increases the total abundance of photoproducts formed. This results from both the strong stellar Lyman-$α$ flux and resonant scattering by the overlying hydrogen layer, which redistributes photons and directs a fraction toward the dust layer. Turbulent mixing and fragmentation cycles further redistribute grains vertically, allowing photoproducts to form throughout the disk. We find that stellar Lyman-$α$ irradiation can produce sufficient photoproducts to produce an organic mantle with an {organic-to-rock ratio of $\approx 10\%$}, which could enhance grain sticking. Our results suggest that FUV-driven organic synthesis may facilitate dust growth and contribute to overcoming barriers to planetesimal formation.

Planetesimal Delivery to Giant Planet Circumplanetary Disks: Bulk Ice Enrichment and an Ice-rich Iapetus

Ignacio Mosqueira · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过模拟微行星在气体盘中的轨迹演化和差异烧蚀过程，揭示了冰质和岩质碎片的选择性捕获机制如何解释木星和土星卫星系统的冰丰富特征。研究发现，不同粒度的外太阳系分化天体碎片在通过行星周盘时，由于烧蚀和碰撞过程的差异，可在不同轨道距离产生0.4-0.9的冰质量分数梯度分布，足以为Iapetus等冰富卫星提供充足的冰质物质。同时考虑卫星体小行星随后的向内迁移会进一步改变卫星的最终组成。
英文原摘要
The regular satellites of Jupiter and Saturn are substantially enriched in ice relative to the rock-rich solids of the outer Solar System. In addition, the satellites interior to Titan and distant Iapetus are icier than Titan itself. Collisional processes and subsequent migration can readily alter compositions deep in the planetary potential well, but producing a distant, ice-rich satellite with Iapetus's radius of about 700 km is a challenge \citep{mosqueira2026a}. We show that differential ablation and capture of icy and rocky fragments from differentiated outer Solar System bodies can explain the bulk Jovian and Kronian satellite ice enrichment and supply the substantial ice-rich inventory needed for Iapetus. For 1\,m--1\,km fragments with the canonical size-distribution index \(p=3.5\) and a source containing 30\% ice by mass, the fixed radial distance Saturn calculation deposits material with ice fractions of 0.66 (delivery of vapor-only rock) or 0.50 (with retained rock melt) at 20 Saturn radii and nearly pure ice at 60 Saturn radii. Following three-dimensional trajectories over repeated passages through the pressure-supported gas gives ice fractions of about 0.4 near 20 Saturn radii and 0.9 near 60 Saturn radii. The matching Jupiter calculation yields a similar bulk ice enrichment, with a corresponding compositional gradient across the Galilean satellite region. These delivery profiles supply ice-enriched material for satellite formation. Yet satellitesimal inward migration subsequently modifies the material incorporated into satellites and thus alters their bulk compositions. We briefly discuss the implications for the PDS~70 planetary system.

JWST Reveals Refractory-rich Water Ice in Interstellar Comet 3I/ATLAS: Evidence for a Continuum of Grain Properties across Protoplanetary Disks

Silvia Protopapa, Michael S. P. Kelley, Martin A. Cordiner, Stefanie N. Milam et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用JWST/NIRSpec对星际彗星3I/ATLAS进行了8个月跨度的光谱观测,发现其水冰成分呈现出异于太阳系彗星的特征——强3μm吸收伴随弱的1.5和2.0μm水冰带,光谱建模表明这源于含有难熔物质的亚微米至微米级水冰聚集体。研究表明,不同原行星盘中形成的类地行星冰质构块中的水冰物理状态可能存在连续统一体,从纯冰颗粒到含不同难熔物质的水冰聚集体,而3I所观测的颗粒性质位于这一连续体中难熔物质较丰富的一端。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present JWST/NIRSpec PRISM observations of the interstellar comet 3I/ATLAS obtained on 2025 August 6 (Epoch 1), 2025 December 22 (Epoch 2), and 2026 April 1 (Epoch 3), spanning 8 months around perihelion at heliocentric distances of 3.3, 2.4, and 5.7 au, respectively. The spectra reveal a broad 3 μ m absorption band together with H 2 O, CO 2 , and CO gas emission. Unlike previously reported water-ice-rich solar system comae, the strong 3 μ m absorption is accompanied by weak or absent 1.5 and 2.0 μ m water-ice bands. Spectral modeling indicates that the observations are best reproduced by submicron- to micron-sized water-ice-bearing aggregates containing refractory material. Compared with Epoch 1, the Epoch 3 spectrum favors the presence of a second population of larger, micron-sized, ice-rich aggregates and exhibits a subtle Fresnel-like structure near 3.1 μ m, consistent with crystalline water ice. The observations can be explained by either crystalline water ice at both epochs, with the spectral evolution arising primarily from changes in grain size and refractory mixing, or an evolution from an amorphous-like to crystalline state. The spectral properties of 3I bridge those of water-ice-bearing solar system comae and several spectral classes of mid-sized trans-Neptunian objects. These similarities suggest that the icy building blocks of planetesimals formed in different protoplanetary disks may share a common continuum in the physical state of water ice, ranging from pure ice grains to water-ice-bearing aggregates with varying refractory content at submicron-to-micron scales, with the grains observed in 3I lying toward the refractory-rich end of this continuum.

Nicola Pietro Gentile Fusillo, Felipe Lagos-Vilches, Boris T. Gänsicke, Christopher J. Manser et al. · Nature Astronomy
本研究发现了白矮星WD J132800.85–145019.95周围罕见的双气体碎屑盘系统,通过Ca II三重线的四峰发射线谱揭示了两个分离的气体环结构。研究表明这种双环构型由两个行星体(尺度从几公里到冥王星大小)分别嵌入其中的碎屑盘所产生,提供了行星在白矮星演化阶段幸存的直接证据。该稳定的双盘结构对现有碎屑盘形成与演化模型构成新的约束,为理解行星系统在宿主星演变中的动力学演化提供了重要案例。
英文原摘要
Abstract Many planetary systems survive the metamorphosis of their host stars into white dwarfs, as demonstrated by the detection of dusty circumstellar discs around these stellar remnants and chemical signatures of planetary debris in atmospheres of these stars. A small fraction of these circumstellar discs also contain gas, detected in the form of emission lines from various ionic transitions (most commonly the near-infrared Ca II triplet). All of the 21 known gas discs exhibit double-peaked line profiles characteristic of a rotating flat disc. The mechanism behind the generation of gas is still debated, but for at least one system it has been linked with the presence of a surviving planetesimal embedded within the dust disc. Here we report the discovery of WD J132800.85–145019.95, a new gas-disc system that exhibits quadruple-peaked instead of double-peaked line profiles. These emission lines originate from two separate gaseous rings extending from 0.14 R ⊙ to 0.34 R ⊙ and from 0.54 R ⊙ to 1.93 R ⊙ . We propose that this structure results from the presence of two distinct planetary bodies within these discs, ranging in size from a few kilometres to the size of Pluto. The detection this stable double-disc presents a new challenge for the current models of debris disc formation and evolution.

High-resolution Modeling of Coronae and Winds in Solar-type Stars with Varying Rotation Rates. II. Stellar Winds

Yue-Hong 悦虹 Chen 陈, Julián D. Alvarado-Gómez, Xin Cheng, Victor See et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用全球对流动力学生成的磁场图和Alfvén波加热模型,对五颗不同自转速率(太阳速率的1.0-23.3倍)的太阳型恒星进行高分辨率风模型计算。研究发现恒星自转越快,螺旋磁场缠绕越紧,Alfvén表面越大,终端风速越高,对系外行星的风压环境更恶劣;质量损失率与表面X射线通量存在幂律关系(幂指数~0.67),提供了自洽的活动-风耦合关系。这些结果揭示了多尺度磁场在恒星风中的作用机制,为宜居区空间天气评估提供了物理约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract Observations suggest a connection between steady-wind mass loss and coronal X-ray activity in low-mass main-sequence stars. Interpreting this connection is challenging because the wind is controlled mainly by the large-scale open field, whereas X-ray emission traces heating in small-scale closed fields often unresolved in global wind models. Here we use Space Weather Modeling Framework–Alfvén-Wave Solar Model with global convective dynamo-generated magnetic maps and solar magnetograms. We model Alfvén-wave-heated winds for four solar-type stars plus the Sun, spanning rotation rates of 1.0–23.3 times the solar rate, and magnetic field strengths of 6.0–1200 G. Our models show that faster rotation yields a more tightly wound spiral, a larger Alfvén surface, higher terminal wind speeds, and a harsher wind-pressure environment for orbiting exoplanets, different from that of the present-day Sun. We estimate the mass- and angular-momentum-loss rates and find systematic differences from Zeeman–Doppler imaging based predictions. Building on the successful reproduction of X-ray coronae in our Paper I, we obtain the first self-consistent activity–wind relation in a unified modeling framework: the mass-loss rate scales with the surface X-ray flux following a power law with an index of ∼0.67. We also reexamine magnetic braking via open-flux magnetization, finding that the effective Alfvénic lever arm depends on the magnetization parameter with a power-law index of ∼0.35. Finally, we quantify the stellar wind pressure at the orbits of several super-Earths. Together with our Paper I, the series of results shows the distinct roles of multiscale magnetic fields and provides physically grounded inputs for assessing habitable-zone space weather.

Propagation of a quasi-stationary zonal wavenumber-2 Rossby wave in Jupiter's stratosphere

Shenghan Ma, Jialin Zhang, Dexin Lai, Patrick Irwin et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过分析卡西尼号CIRS观测数据，识别了木星平流层中的准静止纬向波数-2罗斯贝波，利用垂直相位结构、折射指数计算和Eliassen-Palm通量诊断方法分析其三维结构和传播特性。研究发现该波动在西风导管内向上传播，在准四年振荡(QQO)的东风相位附近发生极向折射，EP通量汇聚区表明波动向背景流输送动量，揭示了行星罗斯贝波与木星QQO之间的动力学耦合机制。这些观测约束对理解具有准周期振荡的行星大气中的波-平均流相互作用具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
Atmospheric planetary waves redistribute energy and momentum and shape the large-scale circulation of giant-planet atmospheres. However, the three-dimensional structure and dynamical role of low-zonal-wavenumber waves on Jupiter remain poorly constrained. We investigate the structure, propagation, and possible dynamical influence of a low-zonal-wavenumber wave in Jupiter's upper troposphere and stratosphere, with a particular emphasis on its coupling to the quasi-quadrennial oscillation (QQO). We analysed four global temperature maps retrieved from Cassini Composite Infrared Spectrometer (CIRS) observations obtained during the 2000--2001 Jupiter flyby. The zonal wavenumber-2 temperature disturbance was isolated from the retrieved three-dimensional temperature field and diagnosed using vertical phase structure, refractive-index calculations, and Eliassen--Palm flux diagnostics. We identify a coherent zonal wavenumber-2 temperature disturbance in Jupiter's northern mid-latitudes that maintains a nearly fixed longitudinal phase over multiple observing epochs, indicating a quasi-stationary Rossby wave. Its vertical phase tilt demonstrates upward propagation, while refractive-index diagnostics show confinement within a favourable westerly waveguide. Eliassen--Palm flux diagnostics further show upward propagation followed by poleward refraction near the turning line associated with the easterly phase of the QQO. Regions of EP-flux convergence indicate momentum deposition into the background flow and suggest that the wave may in turn influence the evolution of the QQO. These results provide observational constraints on the dynamical coupling between planetary Rossby waves and Jupiter's QQO, with broader implications for understanding wave--mean-flow interactions in planetary atmospheres exhibiting quasi-periodic oscillations.

英文原摘要
Direct detection and characterization of exoplanet atmospheres via reflected starlight enables probes of composition of mature exoplanet systems (since flux is decoupled from age) at closer separations than via thermal emission (due to observations in visible wavelengths), and even probes of biosignatures. New and upcoming ground and space-based platforms are enabling these observations for the first time. Recently the 3-m Lazuli Space Telescope, from the Eric and Wendy Schmidt Observatory System, was announced, which will be equipped with the ExtraSolar Coronagraph (ESC) designed for reflected light gas giant exoplanet observations in visible wavelengths. In this paper we present 27 known giant exoplanets that make up the first target list for these observations. Our target list spans ages and temperatures of planets not probed before by direct imaging, but some of which will also be accessible to the \textsl{Nancy Grace Roman} Coronagraph instrument and the ELT-METIS thermal imager, providing comprehensive atmosphere characterization and synergies. We discuss the science enabled by ESC observations of our target list and enhancements provided by future astrometric and radial velocity measurements.

HD 152384: A 5-megayear-old debris disk system showing a gaseous ring of refractory elements at the dust sublimation radius

M. E. van den Ancker, D. P. Iglesias, O. Panić, M. G. Petr-Gotzens et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过UVES和CRIRES高分辨光谱观测5百万年龄的年轻A星HD 152384的周围气体,发现难熔元素气体被限制在距星0.09-0.12 AU的几何薄环中,该位置对应尘埃升华半径。通过建模Keplerian旋转气体并测量Mg I的光学薄谱线,推导出环中含有3.6×10⁻³地球质量的镁气体,其成分和质量与岩质微行星碰撞产生的次生起源一致。研究结论表明HD 152384是β Pic型气体碎屑盘的年轻类似体,为理解岩质微行星碰撞过程中气体和尘埃的早期演化提供了新的观测窗口。
英文原摘要
Circumstellar gas around young stars provides key insights into planet formation and disk evolution. HD,152384, a young (5 Myr) A0,V star in Sco-Cen, exhibits hybrid disk characteristics with a modest infrared excess due to small dust grains and atomic emission lines dominated by refractory elements, but the disk's structure and origin remain unclear. We aim to characterise the kinematics and excitation of the circumstellar gas around HD,152384, and to constrain the physical properties of the emitting region. We used new optical and near-infrared high spectral resolution data from the Ultraviolet and Visual Echelle Spectrograph (UVES) and CRyogenic high-resolution InfraRed Echelle Spectrograph (CRIRES) instruments at the Very Large Telescope to revisit the circumstellar environment of HD,152384. Earlier studies suggested the presence of a broad, gas-rich circumstellar disk; here, we re-examine this scenario. -1 $ peak separation. Modelling of gas in Keplerian rotation indicates that the gas is confined to a geometrically thin ring at a radial distance of 0.09--0.12 au from the central star, close to the dust sublimation radius (0.10--0.14 au, assuming a dust sublimation temperature of ∼1500 K). The detected Fe, i emission comes from an optically thick region. However, the Mg, i lines are optically thin, allowing us to derive a mass of 2.2 times 10^22 kg (or 3.6 times 10^-3 M$_⊕) in magnesium gas confined to the geometrically thin ring. The inferred mass and composition of circumstellar dust and gas in the HD,152384 system are consistent with a secondary origin related to rocky planetesimal collisions. We suggest that the atomic gas seen in our observations is due to the sublimation of small dust grains created in such collisions, spiralling inwards to the dust sublimation radius, creating an accumulation of atomic gas at this location. We conclude that HD,152384 represents a young analogue of gas-bearing debris disk systems such as β Pictoris and of the gaseous debris disks around metal-polluted white dwarfs (in which a similar phenomenology is observed), offering a new window into the early violent phase of rocky planetesimal processing.
planet formation · disk evolution · planetesimal
行星大气1 篇

A Headwind-like Helium Outflow from TOI-3884b

Joe P. Ninan, Jessica Libby-Roberts, Suvrath Mahadevan, Oleg Kochukhov et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过Habitable Zone Planet Finder分光仪对超海王星TOI-3884b进行五次过境观测,在He I 10830 Å谱线检测到6.8±0.27%的过量过境深度,从而探测了其蒸发外层大气。研究发现该行星表现出独特的非对称外流形态——过境前5小时即出现外流信号但过境后几乎无尾流,这种"逆风"现象仅在热土星HAT-P-67b上被观测到。该外流形态支持辐照驱动质量损失机制,可能源于亚Alfvén星-行星相互作用,为利用大气外流观测约束行星磁场和星-行星相互作用理论提供了新途径。
英文原摘要
Constraining atmospheric loss is crucial for understanding the evolution of planetary atmospheres. We report the detection of an evaporating exosphere from TOI-3884b, a super-Neptune in a polar orbit around an M4 dwarf star. We infer this evaporation from a 6.8$\pm$0.27\% excess transit depth in the He I 10830 Å\, line, observed during five transits of TOI-3884b with the Habitable Zone Planet Finder spectrometer. While we observe outflow signatures starting as early as 5 hours before the onset of the planet's optical transit, no significant post-transit tail is detected. Hitherto, such a pre-transit headwind without a significant post-transit tail has only been detected from HAT-P-67b, a hot Saturn orbiting an F-type star. This asymmetric outflow morphology supports irradiation-driven mass loss over core-heated mass loss, and we explore whether this could be caused by sub-Alfvén star-planet interaction. The detection of such complex outflow morphologies is now opening a new window, enabling us to test theories of star-planet interactions and planetary magnetic fields with observations.
atmosphere · atmospheric

热木星与短周期行星1 篇

Demographics of Close-In TESS Exoplanets Orbiting FGK Main-sequence Stars – II. Spectral-Type Dependence and the Neptunian Landscape

Kaiming Cui, David J Armstrong, Andreas Hadjigeorghiou, Marina Lafarga et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究基于TESS数据分析了环绕F、G、K型主序星的近距离行星（0.5–16天轨道周期）的出现率，发现不同谱型主星间的整体行星出现率相近（~10–13颗/百星），但热木星出现率随谱型变化明显（F型0.3%、G型0.5%、K型0.67%）。通过直接在出现率空间中建模海王星沙漠，发现该沙漠在较热的主星周围延伸到更长轨道周期，且K型主星周围的沙漠内行星数量约为F型的两倍，贝叶斯模型比较表明无需引入独立的海王星脊来解释观测数据。这些结果为行星形成和演化理论提供了关键的谱型依赖性约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract Understanding how planetary demographics depend on host star properties is essential for constraining planet formation and evolution theories. In this paper, we analyse close-in (0.5–16 d) planet occurrence rates from the TESS Science Processing Operations Center (SPOC) full-frame image data for individual F-, G-, and K-type main-sequence stellar samples. Integrated over 0.5 &lt; P &lt; 16 d and 2 &lt; Rp &lt; 20 R⊕, the occurrence rates are $12.1^{+3.0}_{-2.3}$, $10.2^{+1.2}_{-1.0}$, and $13.1^{+1.4}_{-1.2}$ planets per 100 stars for F-, G-, and K-type hosts, consistent with each other at 1σ, so any spectral-type dependence is localized rather than global. For transiting hot Jupiters, we measure $0.30^{+0.04}_{-0.03}$, 0.50 ± 0.03, and $0.67^{+0.08}_{-0.07}$ per cent, with the rate peaking at K type; the latter being the first such measurement for K-type hosts. Modelling the Neptunian desert directly in occurrence-rate space, we find that the desert extends to longer orbital periods around hotter stars. Integrating over the desert interior gives $0.069^{+0.045}_{-0.011}$, $0.093^{+0.046}_{-0.022}$, and $0.150^{+0.033}_{-0.026}$ planets per 100 stars, implying about one close-in planet in a hundred lies inside the desert, and the desert is roughly twice as populated around K- as around F-type hosts. A rigorous Bayesian model comparison for the 6–8 R⊕ strip indicates that a distinct Neptunian ridge is not required to explain the measurements.

Searching for substellar companion candidates with Gaia. V. M dwarfs within 15 pc studied with absolute astrometry, radial velocities, and direct imaging

F. Destriez, A. -M. Lagrange, G. Hébrard, F. Kiefer et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过盖亚绝对天体测量、径向速度和直接成像联合分析,对15 pc内582颗M型矮星进行了系统巡天,利用自行异常信号和多波段数据约束搜索亚恒星伴星。研究确认了37颗已知恒星伴星和7颗已知亚恒星伴星,新发现35颗亚恒星伴星(其中至少10颗木星质量行星轨道在0.2-2 au)和77颗伴星候选体,显著扩展了M型矮星周围行星系统的已知种群。这些结果对理解行星形成机制、估算行星发生率以及研究M型矮星周围系统的构型具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
In this work, we survey M-type stars within 15 pc observed by Gaia based on their colour, magnitude, and quality of astrometric data. We aim to detect new substellar companions using primarily Gaia astrometry and, when available, proper-motion anomaly (PMa) along with radial velocity (RV) and direct imaging (DI) data. We used the GaiaPMEX tool to search for bound companions around 582 M dwarfs. We identified all previously known stellar and planetary companions that were consistent with the Gaia excess signal. Then for all newly identified companions, we computed detection limits in the (mass, semi-major axis) parameter space using archival RV and DI data, to possibly rule out the presence of a stellar companion. The joint analysis of the parameter space allowed by astrometry and constrained by RV and DI provides tight constraints on the nature of the companion. We found 156 M dwarfs to have a bound companion within the Gaia sensitivity domain. The astrometric signals of 37 and seven of them are due to known stellar and substellar companions, respectively. We report 35 new substellar companions among which at least ten giant planets orbiting between 0.2 au and 2 au, with masses close to 1 M_Jup. We also report 77 substellar candidates, and provide detection limits using astrometry+RV+DI for 50 stars with known exoplanets and no significant Gaia excess noise. This work reveals a significant population of new substellar companions to M-type stars, challenging core accretion models. These results will be crucial inputs for estimating occurrence rates and studying the architecture of planetary systems around M dwarfs. Some new companions presented here, located beyond the water ice line, are prime targets for DI or coronagraphy observations with JWST or ELT.
direct imaging · radial velocity · astrometry · astrometric · occurrence rate

Uncertainty estimation of incoming comet trajectories with a thermophysical non-gravitational force model for Comet Interceptor encounter planning

Nicholas Attree, Pieter de Boer, Pedro Gutiérrez, Pedro Lacerda et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过蒙特卡罗采样的热物理非引力加速度模型，测试了地基天体测量和标准非引力加速度拟合技术在预测受彗星放气扰动的近日彗星轨迹中的能力。研究发现，由于非引力加速度演化难以预测且天体测量观测存在限制，现有方法难以满足Comet Interceptor任务在撞击前60天内位置和速度精度分别达到1000 km和1 m/s的要求。建议将轨迹不确定度扩大至10,000 km和数个m/s，同时指出更多观测数据（特别是近日点附近的观测）能显著改善非引力加速度参数的反演精度。
英文原摘要
The European Space Agency Comet Interceptor mission is designed to fly-by an as-yet undetermined incoming long-period or dynamically-new comet, which will require an accurate prediction of its trajectory. Cometary orbits are perturbed from purely gravitational trajectories by non-gravitational accelerations (NGAs) stemming from their outgassing, however, and these must be taken into account in the navigation. We here test the ability of ground-based astrometry and standard NGA-fitting techniques to predict the position and velocity of incoming comets perturbed by a Monte Carlo sampled thermophysical acceleration model. We find that, due to the difficulties of predicting NGA evolution as well as the limitations of reasonably simulated astrometry, predicted accuracies are unlikely to meet the mission goals of 1000 km and 1 m s$^{-1}$ in position and velocity, sixty days before intercept. We instead recommend expanded uncertainties of $10,000$ km and several m s$^{-1}$. Our NGA fits struggle to reproduce the reference accelerations and trajectories when outgassing is rising steeply at a few AU-incoming, but with more observations up until perihelion, do eventually converge, leading to good estimates of many of the underlying acceleration parameters even for half an orbital arc.
astrometry

Astrometric detection of rocky exoplanets from inexpensive space platforms

Peter G. Tuthill, George Ayad, Donald G. Dansereau, Thomas Holland et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究探讨了利用低成本空间平台通过星体位置测量法(窄角测量)检测类地系外行星的可行性,针对邻近恒星系统的微角秒级精度测量需求。研究采用模拟方法,对TOLIMAN等小型任务架构进行评估,并提出了搭载超稳定小孔径望远镜(<0.5m)的光学概念设计。结果表明,在数年的专项观测中,即使使用相对廉价的轨道平台,也能够有效搜索Epsilon Eridani和Tau Ceti等邻近恒星周围宜居轨道上的类地行星。
英文原摘要
The detection of orbiting exoplanets by way of astrometric monitoring of the host star to reveal gravitationally induced reflex motion requires extreme precision and measurement stability when targeting Earth-mass objects. Despite this, astrometry is widely regarded as the most promising avenue for the most desired of all targets: true Earth analogue systems in the solar neighbourhood. Formerly thought to require flagship class investment, recently several smaller concepts such as the TOLIMAN and SHERA missions have shown that detections are possible, at least in principle, from more modest space platforms. A key enabling feature is that these missions exploit narrow-angle astrometry. Observing only multiple star systems where the target provides its own astrometric reference greatly alleviates the challenges confronted by traditional astrometry with its reliance on distant, faint field stars. This principle underlies the TOLIMAN mission, for which progress and status is reported. However, only a tiny handful of nearby star systems are suited to such a TOLIMAN-like architecture. This paper discusses speculative optical concepts featuring ultra-stable telescopes of modest (<0.5m) aperture designed for astrometric monitoring of isolated single stars at the micro-arcsecond scales required for the discovery of Earth mass objects orbiting sun-like stars in temperate orbits. In simulation, highly sought-after neighbourhood systems such as Epsilon Eridani and Tau Ceti can be exhaustively searched for rocky planets with dedicated observing campaigns spanning several years, all from relatively inexpensive orbital platforms.
astrometry · astrometric

Ellipsoidal Modeling Framework for TNOs: the triaxial shape of (50000) Quaoar and its implications for Weywot and the dynamical environment

Giuliano Margoti, Felipe Braga Ribas, Eros Gradovski, Gustavo Madeira et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过联合恒星掩食事件和旋转光变曲线数据，开发了一套通用的椭球体建模框架，克服了TNO三维形状测定的几何简并问题。我们将该方法应用于大型TNO Quaoar，确定其为三轴椭球体(a=620.7 km, b=546.5 km, c=511.5 km)，体密度为1.663 g cm⁻³，并进而精确测定其卫星Weywot的质量和密度。动力学模拟表明Quaoar的三轴引力场严格限制了潜在卫星的稳定轨道范围，同时揭示了其环系统中的自旋-轨道共振机制对环结构的调控作用。
英文原摘要
The three-dimensional shapes of Trans-Neptunian Objects (TNOs) are notoriously difficult to determine due to their slow orbital motion and minimal variations in aspect angle over human timescales. To overcome this geometric degeneracy, we developed a versatile modeling framework capable of deriving ellipsoidal shapes by simultaneously combining stellar occultations and rotational light curves, including parameterizations of localized surface albedo. We applied this methodology to the large TNO (50000) Quaoar, jointly modeling 37 stellar occultation events and two photometric datasets. Our MCMC analysis demonstrates that Quaoar is best represented by a triaxial shape with equatorial semi-axes $a = 620.7^{+3.3}_{-2.8}$ km and $b = 546.5^{+6.0}_{-5.6}$ km, and a polar semi-axis $c = 511.5^{+5.3}_{-5.5}$ km, yielding a bulk density of $1.663 \pm 0.127$ g cm$^{-3}$. By isolating Quaoar's barycentric motion from the astrometric offsets, we determined the mass of its satellite, Weywot, to be $(1.485 \pm 0.360) \times 10^{18}$ kg, corresponding to a system mass ratio of $q = 0.0012 \pm 0.0003$ and a satellite bulk density of $0.95 \pm 0.60$ g cm$^{-3}$. Furthermore, dynamical simulations using the derived triaxial gravitational field reveal that potential additional satellites can only maintain stable orbits beyond 2.4 Quaoar radii, as inner bodies would be strongly perturbed. Regarding the ring system, particles at the location of the secondary ring (Q2R) location exhibit libration associated with the 5:7 spin-orbit resonance (SOR). Similarly, particles at the location of the main ring (Q1R) location show libration of the 2:6 SOR angle for orbital eccentricities larger than 0.2, providing a plausible mechanism for the azimuthal width variations observed in Q1R.
astrometric

OGLE-2012-BLG-0563: Relative Proper Motion Constraint from Keck Follow-up finds the correct Lens

Aparna Bhattacharya, David P. Bennett, Jean Phillippe Beaulieu, Ian A. Bond et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过Keck自适应光学成像观测微引力透镜事件OGLE-2012-BLG-0563，利用相对自行方向约束条件进行图像约束的光曲线模型拟合，确定了该行星系统的正确透镜星身份。研究发现该系统由质量约1.12倍木星质量的行星围绕0.81倍太阳质量的主星公转，距离太阳5.49 kpc，透镜-源分离为23.28 mas，这是Keck AO观测中最小的分离。研究揭示了多波段数据中的系统误差对光曲线模型的影响，强调了在未来罗曼望远镜数据分析中需要充分验证系统误差并结合相对自行约束来识别正确的引力透镜。
英文原摘要
We present Keck/NIRC2 adaptive optics imaging results of planetary microlensing event OGLE-2012-BLG-0563. We find that the OGLE-2012-BLG-0563L planetary system consists of a 1.12 +/- 0.09 Jupiter-mass planet orbiting a 0.81 +/- 0.04 solar-mass host star at a distance of 5.49 +/- 0.56 kpc from the Sun. This event has a lens-source separation of 23.28 +/- 0.48 mas, which is the smallest lens-source separation measured to date with Keck AO data. We find that the incorrect solution - which identifies the Northeast (NE) star as the source star - is favored if one only considers the discovery paper, the Fukui et al. (2015) solution, or an unconstrained model. However, by performing an image-constrained light curve model fit using the relative proper motion direction constraint from the Keck follow-up data, we demonstrate that the correct solution identifies the NE star as the lens star. The discrepancy in the unconstrained models is driven by systematics in the FTS and MOA B & C data, alongside the impact of the relative proper motion direction constraints. This outcome offers two important lessons for future Roman data analysis: (1) thoroughly verify systematics across all passbands and identify the precise origin of chi-squared differences, and (2) utilize image-constrained light curve modeling incorporating relative proper motion direction constraints to identify the correct lens.
microlensing

OGLE-2013-BLG-1125/MOA-2013-BLG-584: Discovery of a gas giant orbiting an M dwarf

E. Bachelet, M. Hundertmark, V. Bozza, J. -P. Beaulieu et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过分析OGLE-2013-BLG-1125/MOA-2013-BLG-584微引力透镜事件的多望远镜测光数据，采用微引力透镜光变曲线建模方法，发现了一个质量为105±13地球质量的土星质量行星绕低质量M矮星(质量0.139±0.011太阳质量)运行。该发现为数量稀少的低质量恒星周围巨行星样本增添了新成员，有助于约束行星形成理论。
英文原摘要
We report the discovery and characterization of a planetary system in the microlensing event OGLE-2013-BLG-1125/MOA-2013-BLG-584. We analyze photometric data from OGLE and MOA surveys, as well as LCO, $μ$FUN, and Mindstep follow-up telescopes to model the microlensing light curve. We also used data from the Keck II telescope collected in 2018 to obtain additional constraints on the lensing system. We found a Saturn-mass planet of mass $M_{\rm p}=105\pm13 \ M_\oplus$ orbiting a low-mass host star $M_{\rm L}=0.139\pm0.011 \ M_\odot$ at $1.00\pm0.08$ kpc from Earth. This discovery adds to the small sample of giant planets orbiting low-mass stars and provides constraints on planet formation theories.
