Stage-dependent Superhump Waveform Evolution and Nonstationary Positive-superhump Timing in the Near-period-gap Dwarf Nova YZ Cancri

Zhibin Dai, Xuefei Chen, Zhanwen Han · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究基于TESS连续测光和地面长期观测数据,系统研究了近周期间隙矮新星YZ Cancri的正超晕现象及其演化规律。通过Lomb-Scargle周期分析和精密时间分析发现,正超晕波形在超爆发过程中遵循可重复的阶段相关演化序列(锯齿形→不对称双峰→对称双峰),不同阶段的时间稳定性和振幅与上升/衰减时间存在耦合关系。研究测得的超晕周期P_sh'=0.09043天对应的质量比q≈0.175,表明该系统偏心前动盘的生长、重新分布、稳定化和衰减过程可能驱动了观测到的波形演化,为理解超爆发机制提供了重要约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present a photometric and timing study of the near-period-gap SU UMa-type dwarf nova YZ Cancri, based on nearly continuous Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) photometry and long-baseline ground-based observations. Our main observational result is that the positive-superhump (SH) waveform follows a repeatable, stage-dependent sequence during superoutbursts (SOs). In the two well-covered TESS SOs, and consistently in the long-baseline ground-based SO sample, the plateau waveform evolves from an early sawtooth profile (ST), through a double-humped profile with unequal maxima (DHd), to a more symmetric double-humped profile with nearly similar maxima (DHs). The global and time-resolved Lomb–Scargle periodograms show that power near the positive-SH timescale and its harmonics is concentrated during SOs, whereas quiescent and normal-outburst intervals lack a comparably persistent SH-band signal. The dense TESS maxima–minima timing sequence shows different clock stability in different waveform stages: precursor modulations have slightly longer local periods, the middle-to-late DHs plateau is the most regular timing interval, and the postplateau evolution is affected by phase switching and possible secondary/late-SH contamination. The TESS data also reveal profile-clock coupling, with SH amplitude and rise/decay durations evolving together with the timing residuals. As a secondary timing constraint, we obtain a common TESS-timing-based mean positive-SH period of P sh ′ = 0.09043 ( 27 ) day, corresponding to an SH excess of 4.03(31)% and an approximate mass ratio of q = 0.175(11). The repeatable ST–DHd–DHs sequence makes YZ Cnc a useful system near the lower edge of the period gap and may trace the growth, redistribution, stabilization, and decay of the light source associated with an eccentric, precessing accretion disk during SOs.

The occurrence rate of planets around subgiant stars from the TESS photometric survey

A. Dutta, P. Chaturvedi · Astronomy & Astrophysics

英文原摘要
Subgiant stars represent a critical phase in stellar evolution, offering an opportunity to investigate how planetary systems respond to the early stages of post-main-sequence expansion. Despite their importance, systematic occurrence-rate studies of this population remain sparse compared with those of main-sequence and red-giant hosts. In this paper, we present a uniform analysis of planet occurrence around a sample of 282,904 subgiant stars selected from the Data Release 3 (DR3) catalogue. We performed an independent re-analysis of Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) light curves for transit-signal detection and refinement, followed by multi-stage vetting including a visual inspection and statistical false-positive assessment. We quantified the detection sensitivity of the survey through injection-and-recovery experiments, with 565,808 synthetic transit signals injected across the stellar sample. Over the period range of 1--10 days and the planet-radius range for which the detection completeness is well characterised, we measure a completeness-corrected planet occurrence rate of 0.644^ Gaia +0.048 _ -0.041 %. The occurrence rate is strongly dependent on planet radius, with the highest occurrence contributed by planets in the 1--4,R_⊕ range and a secondary enhancement at 12--15,R_⊕. These results provide a demographic baseline for studying the population of close-in planets around stars undergoing early post-main-sequence evolution.

英文原摘要
Magnetic activity is a result of the interaction between rotation and dynamo processes in cool stars with outer convection zones. Since open cluster members share a common age, distance, and chemical composition, they provide an opportunity for studying the magnetic activity of cool stars in a controlled astrophysical context. We used photometric observations with a cadence of 120 s from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) to study the flare activity of stars in three open clusters with different ages: the Pleiades (100,Myr), the Hyades (700,Myr), and Praesepe (700,Myr). We used the Ohio State Multi-Object Spectrograph (OSMOS) at MDM Observatory with the goal of identifying coronal mass ejections (CMEs) on stars in the Pleiades and Praesepe. We investigated the flaring characteristics of the cluster members using the TESS light curves: we identified the flares and then divided the dataset into different categories based on the effective temperature of the flaring stars and the amplitude of their light curves after flare removal. We calculated the flare energies and investigated the flare frequency distributions of each previously defined group. We studied the dynamic spectra of the Hα line and its environment for the stars of the Pleiades and Praesepe to search for an indication of CMEs. In order to quantify temporal variations in the line strength, we measured the Hα chromospheric activity indices for each spectrum. The power-law indices of the flare frequency distributions obtained in our analysis span the 1.8-2.8 range. We found the highest flare rates in the Pleiades for all investigated spectral types. Our results show that the flare rate of M dwarfs is similar in the Pleiades and the Hyades, while the flare rate for K-type stars in the Pleiades is higher by an order of magnitude. Our analysis reveals that the flare rate in M stars changes with their effective temperature: the rate is highest for T_ ̊m eff ∼3200-3300,K (which coincides with the transition between partially and fully convective stars), with a smooth decrease in flare rate toward cooler and hotter M dwarfs. We detected no CME signals in the OSMOS spectra, and we used this information to place an upper limit on the CME rate.

Edge-on protoplanetary disks provide a spatially resolved view of the vertical distribution of icy planetary building blocks, but inferring their relative abundances requires radiative-transfer (RT) modeling with multiple anisotropic scattering. Each such model costs 10^1-10^3 CPU hours, which precludes principled Bayesian inference and homogeneous, population-scale analyses. Assessment of the Mass Loss and Radius Change of 3I/ATLAS Based on Observed Production Rates

Tessa T Frincke, Darryl Z Seligman · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究针对星际彗星3I/ATLAS在太阳系内的质量损失和半径变化，基于多波段分光观测的挥发性和难挥发物质的生产率数据，建立了适用于任意轨道上经历质量损失的小天体的半径变化计算框架。研究结果表明3I/ATLAS在近日点附近损失了约1.71-5.24米的表面层（气体贡献），相当于初始质量的0.40-1.25%，考虑尘气比后表面侵蚀深度可超过10米。该方法和数据可用于推断3I/ATLAS彗核内各物质成分的亚表面位置分布，为理解行星形成残骸的热化学演化提供约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract Formed from the debris of planet formation, interstellar comets provide invaluable insights into the chemical compositions of planetary systems outside of our Solar System. Spectroscopic observations of 3I/ATLAS, the third interstellar object, reveal production of numerous volatiles and refractory species throughout its trajectory. In this paper we present a framework to calculate the change in radius of an object on an arbitrary trajectory at any point in its orbit, applicable to any small body experiencing mass loss. We next provide a comprehensive, machine readable table containing volatile and refractory production rates from all reported observations of 3I/ATLAS pre- and post-perihelion. Applying these equations to 3I/ATLAS, we calculate that it has lost ∼1.71–5.24 meters of its surface during its passage through the Solar System from gas alone, corresponding to ∼1010 kg and ∼0.40–1.25% of its initial mass. Considering dust-to-gas ratios from 1–4, we estimate surface erosion down to depths &gt;10 meters. We present these calculations as approximations and lower limits based on discontinuous observational data with varying methodology and instrumentation. Conservative and optimistic estimates were calculated over a range of heliocentric distances defined by the onset of activity in reported observations and the typical onset of sublimation distance for each species, respectively. The reported production rates combined with the change in radius calculation can be used to estimate subsurface locations of various species within the nucleus of 3I/ATLAS.

The Lazuli Space Observatory: Architecture and Capabilities

Arpita Roy, Stuart Feldman, Peter Klupar, John DiPalma et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
Lazuli空间天文台是一个3米口径的空间观测设施,搭载宽视场相机、积分视场光谱仪和高对比日冕仪三个互补仪器,从月球3:1共振轨道运行,能在4小时内响应目标观测,在400-1700nm波段实现衍射极限成像和高对比成像能力。该望远镜主要针对时域多信使天文学、恒星与行星系统和宇宙学三大科学目标设计,特别适合系外行星高对比成像、系外行星大气光谱和系外行星快速表征观测。本文介绍了Lazuli望远镜的初步设计阶段的观测架构和性能指标,为后续在轨验证奠定了基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract The Lazuli Space Observatory is a 3 m aperture astronomical facility designed for rapid-response observations and precision astrophysics across visible to near-IR wavelengths (400–1700 nm bandpass). An off-axis, freeform telescope delivers diffraction-limited image quality (Strehl >0.8 at 633 nm) to three instruments across a wide, flat focal plane. The three instruments provide complementary capabilities; the Wide-field Context Camera (WCC) delivers multiband imaging over a 35 ′ × 12 ′ footprint with high-cadence photometry; the Integral-field Spectrograph (IFS) provides continuous 400–1700 nm spectroscopy at R ∼ 100–500 for stable spectrophotometry; and the ExtraSolar Coronagraph (ESC) enables high-contrast imaging expected to reach raw contrasts of 10 −8 and postprocessed contrasts approaching 10 −9 . Operating from a 3:1 lunar-resonant orbit, Lazuli will respond to targets of opportunity in under four hours—a programmatic requirement designed to enable routine temporal responsiveness that is unprecedented for a space telescope of this size. Lazuli’s technical capabilities are shaped around three broad science areas: (1) time-domain and multimessenger astronomy, (2) stars and planets, and (3) cosmology. These capabilities enable a potent mix of science spanning gravitational-wave counterpart characterization, fast-evolving transients, Type Ia supernova cosmology, high-contrast exoplanet imaging, and spectroscopy of exoplanet atmospheres. While these areas guide the observatory design, Lazuli is conceived as a general-purpose facility with open time for the global community. We describe the observatory architecture and capabilities in the preliminary design phase, with science operations anticipated following a rapid development cycle from concept to launch; quoted performance specifications reflect current design requirements and modeling predictions, pending on-orbit verification.

Quantitative Links between Formation and Atmospheric Composition for Giant Planets

Yayaati Chachan, Ruth Murray-Clay, Jonathan J. Fortney · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究建立了一个定量框架,将巨行星大气成分与其吸积历史相关联,通过发展大气金属丰度与金属质量分数的通用关系、推断金属来源并量化不同来源的吸积质量、以及与行星形成模型预期的比较,系统地从精确的大气丰度测量反演行星的吸积过程。基于行星过量金属来自凝聚前沿卵石漂移和蒸发的假设,该研究还导出了形成行星系统所需最小原行星盘质量的新估计。该工作还提供了开源代码以供同行使用。
英文原摘要
Abstract Precise atmospheric abundances of giant exoplanets are becoming increasingly prevalent, and there is a need to systematically convert these measurements into metrics that inform us about the planets’ accretion history. We present a quantitative framework for relating atmospheric composition to the accretion of metals and primordial gas. This includes (i) new relations that simplify and generalize the connection between atmospheric metallicity and metal mass fraction, (ii) methods for inferring the source of the metals and quantifying the quantity of metals accreted via different sources, and (iii) relating the derived quantities to expectations from formation models. We also derive a new estimate of the minimum disk mass needed to form a planetary system based on the amount of excess metals accreted by a planet, assuming that this excess originates from the drift and evaporation of pebbles at condensation fronts. This Letter is accompanied by an open-source code that implements the methods presented herein.

On the Information Content of Ariel Transmission Spectra: Reassessing the Tier System

Michael Radica, Nicolas B. Cowan, Ryan Cloutier, Leo Yang Wang · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过模拟和大气反演分析，评估了Ariel任务不同观测精度等级（Tier 1-3）对系外行星大气成分制约能力的信息含量。研究发现，对于热土星和温暖海王星等巨行星，Tier 1级别的低精度观测已足以对H₂O和CO₂获得≲1.5 dex的制约，而更高精度的Tier 2和Tier 3观测可逐步改进精度并约束其他分子；对于温度较低的亚海王星，识别CH₄仅需Tier 1精度，但探测CO₂则需至少Tier 2精度。该研究表明Ariel的Tier分级系统需要重新评估，因为Tier 1调查对巨行星大气特征化已具有显著科学价值，但温度亚海王星的观测需求可能过高。
英文原摘要
Abstract The European Space Agency’s Ariel mission will conduct a survey of the atmospheric properties of exoplanets around bright stars. The mission is nominally divided into three tiers. The Tier 1 survey will consist of low-precision observations of ∼1000 planets, with a subset of these included in the higher-precision Tier 2 survey expected to be necessary for atmospheric characterization. Tier 3 will be repeated observations of a small number of benchmark planets. Though previous studies have assessed the ability of Ariel to uncover population-level trends, they have generally presupposed a given tier. Here we interrogate this assumption and assess the information content of Ariel transmission spectra as a function of tier for three benchmark planets: a hot-Saturn, warm-Neptune, and temperate sub-Neptune. We simulate a grid of Ariel transit spectra at different Tiers for each target and use retrievals to assess which chemical species are well constrained. We find that for giant planets like a hot-Saturn or warm-Neptune, Tier 1 quality observations are sufficient for ≲1.5 dex constraints on H 2 O and CO 2 , even in the presence of grey clouds—meaning important chemical insights are already obtainable in the Tier 1 survey. Moving to Tiers 2 and 3 results in an incremental increase in precision as well as other molecules becoming constrainable in certain scenarios (e.g., H 2 S, CO). Tier 1 observations are also sufficient to identify CH 4 in a temperate sub-Neptune, whereas observations with at least Tier 2 precision are necessary for CO 2 . The number of transits necessary to reach this observational S/N, however, may be prohibitive for the inclusion of temperate sub-Neptunes in even the Tier 1 survey.

A Semianalytic Model of the Bouncing Barrier for Protoplanetary Dust Aggregates

Sota Arakawa, Haruto Oshiro, Yuki Yoshida, Kiwamu Yoshii · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过分离碰撞的压缩和分离两个阶段，建立了原行星盘尘埃团块碰撞粘着概率的半解析模型，其中压缩阶段采用弹塑性接触模型，分离阶段采用随机键网络断裂统计描述。模型预测较大的团块更容易反弹，因为其接触区域更可能包含弱键，与数值模拟结果相符。将校准后的模型应用于ALMA观测推断的原行星盘团块，发现预测的反弹势垒与观测的尺度-碰撞速度范围相符，为原行星盘尘埃团块碰撞演化提供了有力的理论框架。
英文原摘要
Abstract Collisional bouncing limits the growth of dust aggregates in protoplanetary disks, but its dependence on aggregate size, collision velocity, and filling factor remains poorly understood. Here we develop a semianalytic model for the sticking probability of colliding dust aggregates. We divide each aggregate collision into two phases: a compression phase and a separation phase. The compression phase is described with an elastoplastic contact model, which determines the maximum contact radius and repulsive energy after compression. The separation phase is treated as fracture of a stochastic network of interparticle bonds, whose fracture energy is evaluated using weakest-link statistics. The model naturally predicts that larger aggregates bounce more readily because larger contact regions are more likely to contain weak bonds. Comparison with distinct element method simulations shows that the model reproduces the simulated sticking–bouncing boundary. Furthermore, applying the calibrated model to moderately porous aggregates inferred from Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array observations of protoplanetary disks, we find that the predicted bouncing barrier passes through the observationally inferred size–velocity range. Thus, our semianalytic model provides a useful framework for predicting the collisional evolution of protoplanetary dust aggregates.
protoplanetary disk

TSD: An Inverse Problem Approach for Recovering the Exoplanetary Atmosphere Transmission Spectrum from High-resolution Spectroscopy

Nikolai Piskunov, Adam D. Rains, Linn Boldt-Christmas · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究提出了一种名为传输光谱分解(TSD)的反演算法，能够从高分辨率地基光谱中恢复系外行星大气传输光谱，无需依赖预先计算的大气模板光谱，而是利用高光谱分辨率和仪器稳定性来区分恒星、行星和地球大气成分。通过对VLT/CRIRES+多个凌星观测的模拟和实际K波段数据的应用验证，该方法成功重建了超海王星型系外行星WASP-107b的传输光谱特征，为行星大气组成的模型无关检测提供了新途径。
英文原摘要
Abstract Our ability to observe, detect, and characterize exoplanetary atmospheres has grown by leaps and bounds over the last 20 yr, aided largely by developments in astronomical instrumentation, improvements in data analysis techniques, and an increase in the sophistication and availability of spectroscopic models. Over this time, detections have been made for a number of important molecular species across a range of wavelengths and spectral resolutions. Ground-based observations at high resolution are particularly valuable due to the high contrast achievable between the stellar spectral continuum and the cores of resolved exoplanet absorption features. However, recovering such features without relying on a theoretical or empirical model of the exoplanet atmosphere remains a major hurdle in data analysis, with traditional methods being limited by both the choice of algorithm used to remove the nonexoplanetary components of the signal, as well as the accuracy of model template spectra used for cross-correlation. Here we present a new algorithm Transmission Spectroscopy Decomposition (TSD) formulated as an inverse problem that reconstructs the planetary transmission spectrum without recourse to an exoplanet-atmosphere template, minimizing the number of assumed and theoretically modelled components. Instead of cross-correlation with precomputed template exoplanet spectra, we rely on high spectral resolution and instrument stability to distinguish between the stellar, exoplanetary, and telluric components and velocity frames in the sequence of absorption spectra taken during multiple transits. We demonstrate the performance of our new method using both simulated and real K -band observations from ESO’s VLT/CRIRES+ instrument and present results obtained from two transits of the highly inflated super-Neptune WASP-107 b, which orbits a nearby K7V star.

A triple-induced merger in solves the proplyd lifetime problem and the curiously young shell of M42

M. J. C. Wilhelm, A. A. Trani, S. Portegies Zwart · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究针对猎户座星云星团中恒星年龄与原行星盘快速光蒸发之间的矛盾以及星云壳动力学年龄偏年轻的问题,提出了一个基于von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai振荡诱导的三体星系并合模型。通过蒙特卡洛模拟,我们表明最大质量恒星数十万年前的并合能够增强恒星光度和星风强度,从而加速星云壳膨胀,同时使系统年龄与观测恒星年龄相协调,该机制有效地解决了原行星盘寿命问题和星云壳年龄悖论。
英文原摘要
A longstanding tension exists between the stellar ages in the Orion Nebula Cluster (ONC) and the rapid external photoevaporation of its protoplanetary discs. More recently, the shell of the Orion Nebula has also been found to be dynamically younger than its stars. We propose a new scenario aimed at resolving both age issues. In our scenario, the most massive star of the ONC, formed from the merger between two binary stars induced by von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai oscillations due to the present-day binary companion This merger, taking place between the last episode of star formation and today, resulted in an increased luminosity, leading to stronger external photoevaporation and accelerated expansion of the shell. We used Monte Carlo simulations to explore both whether a sudden brightening of could reconcile the stellar ages and shell expansion. We also considered whether the assumption of triple progenitors for could result in stellar mergers on the relevant timescale. A sudden brightening of a few hundred thousand years ago can be effective in reproducing today's shell, while maintaining a system age close to the stellar age. However, the required increase in the stellar wind luminosity would be greater than a simple merger would be equipped to explain. At a lower luminosity, the mixing of a relatively greater amount of cool photoevaporative wind material into the hot bubble could result in the required increase, and we identified a lower limit on the required strength of this effect. Additionally, we find that progenitor triples merge readily on timescales that are even shorter than the scenario would require. These mergers could be deferred by increased pre-main sequence tidal effects, rotation, and perturbing encounters with other cluster members. Ultimately, our proposed scenario with a merger driven by von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai evolution successfully resolves two issues: the Orion Nebula's proplyd problem and the shell lifetime problems.

Capture of Interstellar Planetesimals in Protoplanetary Discs in Binary Star Systems

Alia Pollard, Evgeni Grishin · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过大规模数值模拟（3×10⁷个实验）系统调查了星际微行星在双星系统原行星盘中的捕获过程，覆盖环星盘和环双星盘两种配置。研究发现大型微行星（≳50 m）捕获效率在双星分离距离~10 au处最低，与观测到的双星系统中行星发生率下降一致，而紧密双星（~10 au）仍可捕获km级微行星。微行星捕获作为一个辅助通道，能够为双星系统提供固体物质，弥补原位微行星形成的不足。
英文原摘要
Abstract Planet formation requires growth over 12 orders of magnitude in size, from micron-sized dust grains to fully formed planets. The formation of $\sim \rm km$ sized planetesimals is the most challenging stage, where radial drift and fragmentation rapidly remove meter-sized solids from the protoplanetary disc. Overcoming this barrier requires favourable conditions for streaming instability to concentrate solids. These problems are exacerbated in binary star systems, where gravitational perturbations and disc truncation reduce the efficiency of planetesimal formation pathways. Planetesimal capture provides an alternative mechanism where unbound planetesimals are captured by the protoplanetary disc through gas-drag, potentially bypassing the most restrictive growth stages. We investigate this process in circumstellar and circumbinary discs using over 3 × 107 numerical experiments across a range of binary separations. For circumstellar discs, capture of large (≳ 50 m) planetesimals is suppressed below ~50 au, with the strongest reduction at ~10 au, broadly consistent with the observed decrease in planet occurrence in binaries. Tight ($\sim 10\ \rm au$) binaries can still capture $\mathcal {O}(1)$ km-sized planetesimals, increasing to $\mathcal {O}(10)$ km-sized in wider systems during the birth cluster’s lifetime. Circumbinary discs are much less efficient, with capture rates dropping to ~1% of the single-star case for binaries above ~1 au and becoming negligible for large planetesimals at wider separations. Overall, planetesimal capture provides a supplementary channel for delivering solids to binary systems and mitigating inefficiencies in in-situ planetesimal formation.
planet formation · protoplanetary disc · planetesimal

The GAPS programme at TNG LXXXII. The first atmospheric investigation of the gas giant WASP-13 b at high resolution with GIARPS

D Sicilia, M Basilicata, G Guilluy, G Scandariato et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究采用GIARPS高分辨率光谱仪对膨胀型热木星WASP-13 b进行首次大气观测,通过叠加相关和单线分析方法搜索He I、Hα、Na I、H₂O、CO等物种的大气信号。研究改进了该星系的轨道几何参数(自旋-轨道角λ = 2.7±1.1°),但在可见光和近红外数据中均未检测到显著的大气吸收特征,注入-恢复测试排除了金属丰度≲10倍太阳值且云顶压力≳10 mbar的等温大气模型。结果表明WASP-13 b属于低密度热木星中具有微弱光谱特征的一类,其传输谱的形态很可能由高空云层主导,而非金属富集大气。
英文原摘要
Abstract Low-density transiting gas giant exoplanets are key benchmarks for atmospheric studies. Despite their large-scale heights, many of them remain poorly characterised. We present the first atmospheric investigation at high-resolution of the inflated (Rp~1.4 RJ), hot Jupiter (T~1500 K) WASP-13 b aiming at constraining its atmospheric composition and refine its orbital geometry. We analysed four transits observed with GIARPS at the TNG. Updated stellar and planetary parameters were re-derived from the most precise literature values, and a new Rossiter-McLaughlin analysis was performed to measure the projected spin-orbit angle (λ) and stellar rotational velocity (vsin i⋆). Atmospheric signals were searched using high-resolution spectroscopy, both with cross-correlation and single-line analyses, targeting key atomic and molecular species (e.g. He i, Hα, Na i, H2O, CO). We find a well-aligned orbit (λ = 2.7±1.1○) and vsin i⋆ = 4.5±0.1 km s−1, improving upon previous constraints. No significant atmospheric absorption is detected in either VIS or NIR data. Injection-and-recovery tests exclude an isothermal atmosphere with metallicity [M/H]≲ 10 ×[M/H]⊙ and simultaneously a grey cloud top pressure Pcloud ≳ 10 mbar, under the assumption of chemical equilibrium. WASP-13 b appears to belong to the class of low-density hot Jupiters with weak or featureless transmission spectra, most likely shaped by the presence of high-altitude clouds. Although a highly metal-enriched atmosphere cannot be fully excluded, current evidence disfavours this scenario. Further observations with next-generation facilities will be essential to characterise the atmospheric properties of this challenging target.

New determination of Jupiter’s vertical cloud and haze structure using dual viewing angle MUSE/VLT observations

Charlotte L B Alexander, Patrick G J Irwin, Ashwin S Braude, Emma Dahl · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究利用VLT/MUSE双视角观测和辐射传输算法反演木星的气溶胶垂直结构,通过最小化模型自由参数和同时拟合多个视角数据来克服可见/近红外遥感中的参数简并问题。研究表明木星需要两层云结构(主云层1.4 bar和霾层0.2 bar)才能成功再现2018-2023年的六年观测数据,包括大红斑区域的观测。研究还反演了氨气分布,发现木星的着色物质主要位于主云层,这一云参数化方案可成功复现40°N至40°S全圆盘光谱。
英文原摘要
Abstract Jupiter is a hugely varying and diverse planet, demonstrated through spectacular changes in the cloud top appearance. However, the cloud structures which contributes to this appearance are hard to determine and are not known with certainty. A lack of in-situ observations and many unknown parameter values and numerous near-degenerate pairings within the parameter space for visible/near infrared observations of the aerosol profiles makes the problem under constrained. This work utilises several methods to alleviate these issues. Fitting simultaneously to dual viewing angle VLT/MUSE observations, using a radiative transfer algorithm, provides greater constraint to the aerosol profile than fitting to a single viewing geometry alone. To reduce the degeneracy between parameter pairings in the model, we utilise an aerosol profile with a minimised number of variable parameters by predetermining these values wherever possible. We find two clouds are needed, with a main cloud and haze layer fixed at 1.4 and 0.2 bars respectively, to successfully reproduce six years of VLT/MUSE observations (2018 - 2023), including dual angle observations of the Great Red Spot. We also retrieve an ammonia gas distribution across the disc similar to previous derived profiles, including the peak in deep volume mixing ratio at 5°N before a rapid depletion at 10°N. This cloud parameterisation successfully reproduces spectra extracted from 40°N to 40°S across the disc of Jupiter. For all locations (including the GRS) we find the material responsible for colouring Jupiter to be located in the main cloud layer (1.4 bar).

JWST/MIRI Imaging Search for Kinematically Detected Protoplanetary Candidates

Gabriele Cugno, Myriam Benisty, Richard Teague, Anthony Boccaletti et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过JWST/MIRI冠状光学成像观测五个原行星盘，试图直接探测之前由气体运动学推断存在的嵌入式原行星候选体。采用参考点扩展函数减法结合内部盘发射前向建模的方法改进了小分离度伴星的灵敏度，但在所有系统中都未检测到与原行星一致的点源，而是在三个系统中探测到11.3 μm的延展外盘辐射。观测得到的质量上限（几百AU处为3-20 M_J）仍高于气体运动学推断的质量（~1-5 M_J），主要受到内外盘辐射贡献的限制，凸显了用JWST/MIRI在大分离度观测形成环境中嵌入式原行星的挑战。
英文原摘要
Abstract Kinematic perturbations observed with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array in CO line emission provide evidence for a population of embedded giant protoplanets shaping the structure of protoplanetary disks. We present JWST/MIRI F1140C ( λ = 11.3 μ m) coronagraphic observations of five protoplanetary disks—HD 163296, RXJ 1615.3–3255, RXJ 1842.9–3532, SY Cha, and LkCa 15—with the goal of directly detecting candidate protoplanets orbiting at ≳70 au that were previously inferred from gas kinematics. The data were analyzed using a bespoke methodology that combines subtraction of a reference point-spread function with forward modeling of partially resolved inner disk emission, which otherwise dominates the diffraction pattern in the images. This approach improves the sensitivity to young companions at small separations. No point source consistent with an embedded protoplanet is detected in any of the systems. Instead, in three systems we detect extended emission at 11.3 μ m tracing the outer disk out to radii comparable to those probed by CO. Injection tests indicate upper mass limits of roughly 3–20 M J at separations of a few hundred astronomical units, assuming no additional thermal contribution from the circumplanetary environment. Even with space-based observations, these limits remain mostly above the ∼1–5 M J masses inferred from disk kinematics, largely due to the limitations imposed by emission (and/or scattered light) contributions from both the inner disk and outer disk. These observations highlight the challenges of observing protoplanets embedded in their forming environment at large separation with JWST/MIRI. Lessons learned can inform future studies with the Extremely Large Telescope, which will probe separations where the occurrence rate of gas giants is expected to be higher.

The Sun’s Chemical Peculiarity: Disentangling Galactic Chemical Evolution and Planetary Engulfment in Solar Twins

Mia Babatsikos, Fan Liu, Amanda Karakas, Ilya Mandel et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究对79颗太阳孪生星的高分辨光谱进行了差分光谱学分析,利用贝叶斯框架区分了银河化学演化和行星吞噬对太阳化学组成的影响。研究获得了18个元素的高精度丰度(精度~0.015 dex),发现太阳相对于平均太阳孪生星的化学特殊性主要由银河化学演化驱动(样本中62.3%的星体符合GCE趋势),同时识别出2-6颗候选太阳孪生星可能存在行星吞噬信号。这些结果表明在解释太阳孪生星丰度模式时必须考虑银河化学演化效应,且太阳相对于大多数孪生星可能并非化学异常。
英文原摘要
Abstract Recent observational studies have suggested that the Sun may be chemically peculiar relative to the majority of solar twins. Here, we reanalyze high-resolution, high signal-to-noise spectra of 79 nearby solar twins using a differential spectroscopic approach and Bayesian framework to test whether the Sun’s chemical peculiarity arises from Galactic chemical evolution (GCE) or planetary ingestion. Using the spectroscopic tool Korg , we obtain highly precise, validated atmospheric parameters and abundances for 18 elements, with an average abundance precision of 0.015 dex (3.5%). Employing an independent Bayesian indicator, we disentangle GCE and planetary engulfment signatures from other processes influencing stellar composition, including intrinsic abundance scatter. Our results indicate that the chemical peculiarity of the Sun relative to the average solar twin is largely driven by GCE effects, with 62.3% ± 5.8% of our sample exhibiting abundance patterns well-described by GCE trends. We further identify 2–6 solar-twin candidates exhibiting chemical signatures consistent with planetary engulfment that warrant further investigation. These findings reinforce the importance of accounting for GCE effects when interpreting solar-twin abundance patterns, and suggest that the Sun may not be chemically peculiar relative to the majority of solar twins.

本研究利用JWST/NIRCam和LBTI/LMIRCam对年轻M矮星RX J0534.0-0221进行直接成像观测，通过多波段测光和16个月后的跟踪观测确认了一颗质量约2.8 M_Jup、距主星~14 au的巨行星，并检测到了碎片盘结构。大气模型拟合表明该行星大气中可能存在非平衡化学或增强的金属丰度，这为M矮星周围巨行星的形成机制研究提供了关键观测证据。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present the discovery of RX J0534.0-0221 b, a giant planet orbiting an M dwarf star in the β Pictoris moving group. RX J0534 was originally observed with JWST/NIRCam in the F444W and F200W filters. Observations in F444W reveal a point source at signal-to-noise ratio ∼17.5 at ∼ 0 . ″ 41 (∼14 au) from the host star, with no detection of the source in F200W. A follow-up observation with LBTI/LMIRCam in the L ′ band redetects the source 16 months after the JWST epoch, providing evidence for common proper motion over a chance alignment with a background interloper at the 6 σ –7 σ level. Atmospheric grid model fits to the available photometry yield the bolometric luminosity log 10 ( L / L ⊙ ) = − 5.4 8 − 0.19 + 0.10 dex. At an age of 18–26 Myr, hot-start evolutionary models predict M = 2 . 8 − 0.5 + 0.5 M Jup and T eff = 67 4 − 49 + 57 K. From the L ′ − F 444 W color and magnitudes, we find evidence for disequilibrium chemistry or enhanced metallicity in the planet's atmosphere. Additionally, an extended structure is detected in the JWST F200W observation, consistent with a resolved debris disk with a peak density radius of 7 9 − 3 + 3 au and an inclination of 56 . ° 5 − 1.5 + 1.5 RX J0534 b is one of the lowest-mass planets imaged to date. After TWA 7 b, it is the second imaged planet around an M dwarf orbiting at solar system scales (the first within 40 au) and the first to be confirmed via common proper motion. Future orbital monitoring and atmospheric characterization will shed light on its formation history, a particularly interesting question given the challenging nature of giant planet formation around M dwarfs.

Metallicity does not reorder line-to-line sensitivity to granulation in solar-like stars

Dušan Vukadinović, Sowmya Krishnamurthy, Valeriy Vasilyev, Veronika Witzke et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过三维磁流体动力学模拟在不同金属丰度条件下恒星肉粒层的光谱效应，定量分析了中性和电离谱线对肉粒层扰动的敏感性随金属丰度的变化规律。虽然金属丰度增加会增强对流强度并系统性地提高谱线对肉粒层的敏感性，但不同谱线间的相对敏感性排序在不同金属丰度和视角下保持稳定。这一发现表明，为减小肉粒层诱发的径向速度误差，可以直接将太阳光谱下的谱线掩膜应用于非太阳金属丰度但其他参数相同的恒星，从而提高系外行星探测的精度。
英文原摘要
Stellar granulation imprints line-dependent wavelength shifts and profile variations that limit the precision of radial velocity (RV) measurements used for exoplanet detection and characterization. Understanding how the sensitivity of individual spectral lines to granulation depends on stellar parameters is therefore essential for mitigating granulation-induced RV signals. Here, we investigate the effect of metallicity using MURaM 3D magnetohydrodynamic simulations of stellar granulation including a small-scale dynamo for metallicities $\mathrm{M/H}=\{-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.3,0.5\}$. We synthesize spectra of neutral and ionized lines spanning a broad range of lower excitation potentials and transition probabilities using the MPS-ATLAS code, assuming local thermodynamic equilibrium. We quantify the sensitivity to granulation using the continuum-intensity-weighted standard deviations of the line centre-of-gravity wavelength and equivalent width, denoted by $σ_λ$ and $σ_W$, respectively. Both metrics increase systematically with metallicity at disc centre owing to the combined effects of more vigorous convection and metallicity-dependent changes in the line opacity. We find that the correlation between the solar ($\mathrm{M/H}=0.0$) and non-solar sensitivity metrics remains high from the disk center to the stellar limb. This indicates that the relative ordering of line sensitivities is robust against changes in viewing angle and metallicity. Consequently, line masks calibrated on the solar spectrum can be reliably applied to radial-velocity measurements of stars with non-solar metallicities, provided they have solar effective temperature and surface gravity.

Gravitational Microlensing

Aikaterini Vandorou, Stela Ishitani Silva · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
重力微引力透镜是一种利用行星引力场扰动背景光源来探测系外行星的方法,无需直接探测宿主星或行星的光。该方法相较其他技术具有独特优势,能够探测宽轨道行星、不同质量行星和雪线外的行星,从而补充对近轨或高质量行星更敏感的探测手段。通过从微引力透镜光变曲线和高角分辨率观测中提取物理参数(如质量和距离),该方法对约束宽轨道和雪线外行星至关重要,有助于揭示行星形成机制和太阳系起源。
英文原摘要
Gravitational microlensing is a unique planet-detection method that does not rely on detecting light from the host star or planet. Instead, planets are discovered when their gravitational field perturbs the light coming from a distant source. The mechanics of this method offer several advantages. It has the ability to detect wide-orbit planets of various masses around host stars of different types, at and beyond the system's snow line. Thus, microlensing complements the other detecting techniques that are more sensitive to close-orbit or high-mass planets. In this Chapter, we review the microlensing method and discuss how the physical properties, such as mass and distance, can be extracted from the microlensing light curve or from additional high-angular resolution observations. Microlensing is essential for the broader context of exoplanet demographics, as it can place constraints on planets, in wide orbits, beyond the {\it snow line}. Studying this parameter space can help us unravel the details of planet formation and how our own Solar System formed. Lastly, we provide an example of how to model a light curve and obtain accurate physical parameters for the host and planet.
microlensing · exoplanet demographics

EK Cha b: multi-tracer detection of a protoplanet candidate in a gas-rich environment

Felipe Alarcón, C. Swastik, Stefano Facchini, Edwin A. Bergin et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过多波段观测（CO发射线、毫米连续谱和0.82微米测光）在晚型M矮星EK Cha周围检测到原行星候选体EK Cha b，并利用HST、VLT和ALMA等多个档案数据进行表征和物理性质分析。观测证据表明EK Cha b具有与环行星盘相符的CO J=2-1谱线特征，演化模型预测其为行星质量天体。本工作表明低质量宿主星系统已成为原行星搜索的可达范围，为未来针对M矮星的系统性原行星巡测提供了新的机遇。
英文原摘要
Despite sustained community efforts and advances in instrumentation and high-contrast imaging, the search for protoplanets remains difficult, with only two widely accepted systems to date in Class II systems (PDS 70 and WISPIT 2). Here we present the detection of EK Cha b, a new protoplanet candidate, identified through CO emission, millimeter continuum and 0.82 micron broadband photometry, linked to EK Cha, a young late-type M star. The detection of a new protoplanet candidate offers an additional opportunity to advance our understanding of planet formation. By exploiting the wealth of archival data available for nearby star-forming regions, we aim to place EK Cha b in context, characterize its properties, and motivate future campaigns targeting similar systems. We analyzed high-spatial-resolution archival observations of EK Cha from HST, VLT/NACO, VLT/MUSE, and ALMA. We searched for EK Cha b in broadband continuum and gas tracers, reporting the measured flux with uncertainties, and upper limits for non-detections. EK Cha b shows a spectrally-resolved footprint that matches the CO J=2--1 emission, together with 0.82 micron and millimeter continuum emission. EK Cha b is spectrally resolved in CO J=2--1 emission, kinematically consistent with a circumplanetary disk. Best fits from evolutionary models place EK Cha b as a planetary mass companion, although deeper observations are required to fully confirm its nature. Although giant planets are uncommon around M dwarfs, these stars host the highest planet occurrence rate while remaining severely underrepresented in protoplanet searches. EK Cha b demonstrates that such systems are now within reach, and the advent of extremely large telescopes and improved high-contrast imaging -- where the lower host luminosity relaxes the required contras-- will open this overlooked regime to systematic exploration.

Starspot Activity and Third-body Perturbations in the Eclipsing Binary KIC 9451096

Supattra Wongsriya, Siramas Komonjinda, Puji Irawati, Suwicha Wannawichian · The Astronomical Journal
本研究利用Kepler和TESS空间测光数据及地面观测，通过分析超过2000次日食时间变化来研究食双星系统KIC 9451096。结合光行时效应模型和离散傅里叶变换分析，揭示了三个特征时间尺度：1.25天轨道周期、~85天的第三体摄动周期（对应最小质量0.0487太阳质量的褐矮星伴星）和~5.2天的磁活动周期。通过PHOEBE软件的光曲线拟合表明，次星上演变的黑子活动可解释观测到的O'Connell效应，支持该系统为包含褐矮星伴星的分层三体系统，同时磁活动对短时间尺度的测光和时间变化有重要贡献。
英文原摘要
Abstract We investigate the short-period Algol-type (EA-type) eclipsing binary system KIC 9451096 using high-precision space-based photometry from Kepler and the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) , complemented by ground-based observations from the Sierra Remote Observatories (SRO), which provide additional constraints on system properties. Eclipse timing variations (ETVs) are analyzed using more than 2000 eclipse timings, consisting of approximately 1000 primary and 1000 secondary minima. The timing data are examined using the light-travel time effect (LTTE) model and discrete Fourier transform (DFT) techniques to search for periodic modulations in the O – C diagram. The combined eclipse-timing and frequency analyses reveal three characteristic timescales: the 1.25 day orbital period, a dominant ∼85 day modulation consistent with LTTE induced by a tertiary companion, and secondary ∼5.2 day variability that is most plausibly associated with magnetic activity. The LTTE solution yields a minimum tertiary mass of M 3 ≈ 0.0487 M ⊙ (∼51 M Jup ), assuming a total mass of 1.83 M ⊙ for the eclipsing pair. This is consistent with a brown dwarf in a nearly circular orbit. Light-curve modeling with PHOEBE 2.4.21 demonstrates that evolving starspots on the secondary component can reproduce the observed O’Connell effect. This supports the interpretation that magnetic activity contributes to the observed ∼5.2 day modulation. The LTTE and eclipse-timing analyses support the scenario in which KIC 9451096 is a hierarchical triple system containing a brown-dwarf tertiary companion, while the starspot modeling provides a complementary physical interpretation of the observed short-timescale photometric and timing variability associated with magnetic activity.

Two Low-Mass-Ratio Microlensing Planets from High-Magnification Events: KMT-2021-BLG-0247 and MOA-2023-BLG-169

Kansuke Nunota, Andrew Gould, Takahiro Sumi, Ian A. Bond et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
我们分析了两个高放大微引力透镜行星事件KMT-2021-BLG-0247和MOA-2023-BLG-169，通过有限源效应、微引力透镜视差和Euclid后续成像等多种约束方法，成功确定了低质量比行星系统的物理参数。KMT-2021-BLG-0247具有较强的参数约束，得到主星质量$M_L=0.9\pm0.1\,M_\odot$和行星质量$M_p=41\pm5\,M_\oplus$；而MOA-2023-BLG-169由于源星极暗（$I=26.73$），利用Euclid/VIS数据辅助约束得到主星质量$M_L=0.48^{+0.23}_{-0.17}\,M_\odot$和行星质量$M_p=143^{+103}_{-63}\,M_\oplus$。这两个案例表明，对于极暗源事件，星系模型先验和独立通量约束在确定事件时标和Einstein半径等基本参数时仍具有重要作用。
英文原摘要
We present an analysis of two planetary microlensing events, KMT-2021-BLG-0247 and MOA-2023-BLG-169, both of which exhibit high magnifications and low planet--host mass ratios, but with markedly different levels of parameter constraint. For KMT-2021-BLG-0247, the finite-source effect and microlens parallax are both clearly detected, leading to unusually tight constraints on the physical properties of the lens system. The host mass and lens distance are approximately $M_{\rm L}=0.9\pm0.1\,M_\odot$ and $D_{\rm L}=6.7^{+1.0}_{-0.3}\,\mathrm{kpc}$, respectively, and the planet mass is $M_{\rm p}=41\pm5\,M_\oplus$. The projected planet--host separations are $a_\perp=3.4^{+0.7}_{-0.3}\,\mathrm{au}$ and $3.0^{+0.6}_{-0.3}\,\mathrm{au}$ for the wide and close solutions, respectively. In contrast, MOA-2023-BLG-169 involves an extremely faint source, with $I_{S,{\rm OGLE}}=26.73$, such that the light curve permits a broad family of strongly correlated solutions. We therefore construct the physical-parameter likelihood using approximate invariant combinations of the light-curve parameters and supplement it with post-event Euclid/VIS imaging from the Euclid Q2 Galactic Bulge Survey (Beaulieu et al. 2026). The Euclid data reveal a component at the event position with ${\rm VIS}_{\rm AB}=23.05\pm0.09\,{\rm mag}$. Interpreting this component as the combined light from the source and any luminous lens, the resulting posterior gives $M_{\rm L}=0.48^{+0.23}_{-0.17}\,M_\odot$, $D_{\rm L}=3.50^{+2.82}_{-1.20}\,\mathrm{kpc}$, $a_\perp=1.62^{+0.77}_{-0.43}\,\mathrm{au}$, and $M_{\rm p}=143^{+103}_{-63}\,M_\oplus$. MOA-2023-BLG-169 demonstrates that, for an extreme faint-source event, even the event timescale and angular Einstein radius can remain strongly dependent on the Galactic prior and independent flux constraints.

Astrometric planet search around southern ultracool dwarfs: V. Discovery of the exoplanet candidate PALTA-1 b

J. Sahlmann, P. F. Lazorenko, E. L. Martín, D. Ségransan et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过8年以上的长期精密天体测量监测，利用VLT/FORS2观测附近早L型超冷矮星DENIS-P J1253108-570924的位置偏差，发现了亚木星质量的系外行星候选体PALTA-1 b。该行星估计质量为0.54-0.77木星质量，轨道分离为1.6-3.4天文单位，是已知环超冷矮星运行的极少数巨行星之一。这一发现完成了针对20颗附近超冷矮星的大规模天体测量行星搜索调查。
英文原摘要
We report the discovery of PALTA-1 b, a sub-Jupiter mass exoplanet candidate orbiting the nearby early-L dwarf DENIS-P J1253108-570924 (DE1253-57) located at a distance of ~17 pc. The exoplanet was discovered by monitoring the L dwarf's astrometric motion with FORS2 at the Very Large Telescope over more than eight years and characterising the deviations from the expected single-source motion. The estimated planet mass is between 0.54 MJup and 0.77 MJup, depending on the uncertain mass of the ultracool dwarf host. The system's mass ratio is about 0.010, and the relative separation is estimated at 1.6-3.4 au. If confirmed, PALTA-1 b would be one of the very few known examples of a giant planet orbiting a nearby ultracool dwarf. This discovery finalises a larger astrometric planet search survey that targeted 20 nearby ultracool dwarfs and previously discovered three new ultracool binary systems.

Characterization of the Compact Multiplanet System TOI-789: Validation of Three Terrestrial Planets, Dynamical Characterization, and Detection of a Fourth Temperate Candidate

Juan F. Pagés Navarrete · The Astronomical Journal
本研究利用7年TESS光度测量、地基观测和高分辨率成像,通过TRICERATOPS统计方法验证了M矮星TOI-789周围三颗类地行星(b、c、d),并通过迭代凌星最小二乘法发现了第四颗温度较温和的行星候选体。动力学分析(SPOCK和N体积分)表明该系统在近共振态且动力学冷的配置下仅在低离心率条件下稳定,离心率而非行星质量是系统稳定性的主要限制因素。该研究展示了围绕M矮星的紧凑类地行星系统这一数值上占优的天体种群,其大气表征受限于主星亮度,但可通过动力学表征获得互补认识。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present the validation and dynamical characterization of TOI-789 (TIC 300710077), a compact multiplanet system around an M3 dwarf at 43 pc. Using ∼7 yr of TESS photometry (42 sectors), public ground-based follow-up (LCOGT, MEarth), and high-resolution imaging (Gemini/Zorro, SOAR), we statistically validate three terrestrial planets with TRICERATOPS: TOI-789 b ( R p = 1.18 R ⊕ , P = 5.45 days), c (1.15 R ⊕ , 8.04 days), and d (1.40 R ⊕ , 12.97 days), with false-positive and nearby-false-positive probabilities below the validation thresholds once resolved neighbours are excluded using the on-target ground-based photometry. Through an iterative Transit Least Squares search we identify a fourth, previously uncatalogued planet candidate ( P = 19.74 days, R p ≈ 0.88 R ⊕ ), the outermost and most temperate member ( S = 1.71 S ⊕ , T eq ≈ 292 K); its low signal-to-noise ratio precludes formal validation, so we report it as a candidate pending confirmation. A dynamical analysis (SPOCK and N -body integrations) indicates a dynamically cold, near-resonant system that is stable only for low eccentricities ( e ≲ 0.05); it is eccentricity rather than planetary mass that limits the system's stability, and including the fourth candidate tightens this constraint further. The four objects span the Earth–Venus transition across the Venus zone, with two validated planets falling in the temperate regime under the equilibrium-temperature criterion, making the system a potential comparative-planetology laboratory anchored on confirmed planets. The low TSM (∼3–5) and moderate host brightness place atmospheric characterization beyond reach. TOI-789 exemplifies the numerically dominant population of compact terrestrial-planet systems around M dwarfs whose characterization is limited by host brightness for which dynamical characterization offers a complementary route.

A comparison between particle and fluid approaches for streaming instability in global patch simulations

Dominik Hetzel Ostertag, Mario Flock, Paul Rosendahl, Andrea Mignone et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究在全局盘模型中比较了流体和粒子两种数值方法对流式不稳定性的模拟结果,以确定哪种方法更适合研究行星形成过程中尘埃团块的形成。通过FARGO3D和PLUTO两个代码的二维轴对称模拟,跟踪了尘埃比例高度、中面尘气比和最大尘埃密度等物理量的演化。研究表明流体方法在5120个气体尺度高度分辨率下已收敛到高斯分布特征,而粒子方法尚需更高分辨率以消除瞬态中面尘埃成分,采用更精确的黎曼求解器或高阶重构方案有助于改进收敛性。
英文原摘要
Identifying the process relevant to planet formation is still a major task. Mechanisms have been identified that can trap dust, preventing it from migrating inwards. A particular hydrodynamic instability, known as the streaming instability, is of high interest because it may be responsible for the formation of dust clumps from which the first planetesimals form. We aim to compare the outcome of non-linear streaming instability simulations using both fundamental approaches in a more realistic global disk model. The results will not be used to identify or disentangle the effect of, e.g., numerical diffusion in fluid simulations or the possibility of having crossing particle streams. Instead, they will enable us to determine whether the Lagrangian particle approach is the most suitable for numerical studies of the streaming instability. Methods: We perform 2D, axisymmetric, global, and isothermal hydrodynamic simulations using the two codes FARGO3D (dust fluid) and PLUTO (fluid, particles) to investigate the temporal evolution of dust scale height, midplane dust-to-gas ratio, and maximum dust density as well as to study velocity distributions and vorticities. For PLUTO particle simulations, we also investigate the influence of the gas Riemann solver and spatial reconstruction orders. Our results from a resolution study up to 5120 cells per gas scale height indicate convergence between PLUTO fluid and FARGO3D. Both yield a Gaussian profile dominated by small-scale, dust-depleted voids. Particle simulations with PLUTO have not yet converged, as a transient midplane dust component persists, indicating the need for even higher resolution or more particles. Using a more accurate Riemann solver or a higher-order spatial reconstruction scheme helps to bring the dust density profile closer to the converged structure.

Fast superposition T-matrix method for computing light scattering by structurally diverse aggregates

J. Markkanen · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究针对行星大气和原行星盘中的多孔聚集体粒子散射计算效率问题，提出了改进的快速叠加T矩阵方法（FaSTMM2），采用宽带多层快速多极算法、自适应八叉树结构和分层平面波展开等技术，实现了对不同孔隙度和分形维数聚集体的高效计算。数值验证表明该方法对高孔隙度聚集体的计算速度相比传统方法提高了数个数量级，为行星科学中复杂颗粒系统的光学特性研究提供了重要的计算工具。
英文原摘要
The superposition T-matrix method is widely used to compute the scattering, absorption, and extinction of light by aggregated particles found in cometary comae, planetary atmospheres, and protoplanetary discs. However, commonly used solvers rapidly lose their efficiency with decreasing fractal dimension or with increasing porosity. We present a new open-source implementation of a fast superposition T-matrix method, FaSTMM2, which provides efficient solutions for a wide range of fractal particle aggregates composed of polydisperse monomers. The solver accelerates the superposition T-matrix method using a broadband multilevel fast multipole algorithm. We have implemented diagonal translation operators at super-wavelength levels based on the plane-wave expansion of spherical waves, while sub-wavelength levels are treated using low-frequency stable spherical-wave translators. In addition, we introduced an adaptive octree data structure that enables the efficient treatment of aggregates composed of polydisperse monomers. Numerical examples demonstrate that FaSTMM2 provides an efficient solution with controllable accuracy for a wide range of aggregates and compositions. In particular, it achieves speed-ups of several orders of magnitude for highly porous aggregates with a fractal dimension of around two.

Water-rich sub-Neptunes and rocky super-Earths around different stars: Radii shaped by volatile partitioning, formation, and evolution

Remo Burn, Komal Bali, Caroline Dorn, Rafael Luque et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过耦合行星形成模型（包括行星体和气体吸积、轨道迁移）与演化模型，探讨了富水亚海王星的大气组成、挥发分分馏和水向岩石内核溶解如何共同塑造2-4地球半径行星的质量-半径关系。研究发现，混合成分大气情景最好地再现了系外行星的观测特征，大气损失中的分馏效应在初始条件下影响有限，而水向内核的溶解在不同质量范围表现出不同的重要性。作者量化了质量半径谷的位置及其对恒星质量的依赖性，并指出需要更多统计调查来区分不同演化情景。
英文原摘要
Despite precise characterization measurements, the nature of planets with radii between 2 and 4 Earth radii -- sub-Neptunes -- remains unknown due to degeneracies in interior models. Nevertheless, the field has compiled an impressive ensemble of small planets with measured masses and radii orbiting different stars. This dataset can be used to test the prediction of large water reservoirs on sub-Neptunes, as proposed by planet formation theory with orbital migration. We aim to determine whether this water reservoir is included in photoevaporative winds and how much of it can partition into the rocky and metallic interior. We coupled the results of a planetary formation model with planetesimal and gas accretion as well as orbital migration with evolution models that assume either perfect mixing of water with H/He in the envelope or complete segregation. For the mixed envelopes, we also included an analytic treatment of fractionation during photoevaporative mass loss. Furthermore, we studied the effect of equilibrium dissolution of water into an assumed magma ocean and metallic core for the first time in coupled formation-evolution models. Out of the tested scenarios, the mass-radius relation of exoplanets is relatively well matched by all scenarios where the atmosphere is of mixed composition. The agreement depends on mass, with better consistency for the model without dissolution below 3 Earth masses and hints of the opposite at higher masses. Fractionation is not found to significantly alter the properties of the planets for our initial conditions due to initially massive envelopes on all planets. For all scenarios, we quantified the radius valley location and its scaling with stellar mass. The mass--radius relations for water-rich sub-Neptunes are broadly consistent with observations. However, statistical surveys in both mass and radius are required to distinguish between the scenarios. The mixed-composition loss of different volatiles, their dissolution into the planetary interior, and the solidification of the magma ocean are natural next steps toward a comprehensive atmosphere-interior evolution model.

A Jupiter-like radio aurora in the L3.5 dwarf LSPM J0036+1821

D. Martín-Carrero, J. B. Climent, P. A. Miles-Páez, J. Balmón-Antón et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过VLA射电观测和TESS光学测光对L3.5型超冷矮星LSPM J0036+1821进行多波段长期监测(2019-2023年),检测到具有高圆偏振的持久紧凑射电辐射。通过周期图分析和木星磁层类比模型,我们确定其自转周期为3.079小时,并将射电发射分解为稳定的主椭圆和变化的活跃磁力线分量。研究结果表明该天体的射电辐射源于结构化磁层中电子回旋激射不稳定性驱动的极光过程,与木星类似光环系统相符,可能涉及伴星或内部驱动的磁层相互作用。
英文原摘要
Ultracool dwarfs exhibit radio emission revealing strong magnetic fields and complex magnetospheric processes. Their auroral emission resembles that observed in magnetized Solar System planets, providing a framework to investigate their magnetic topology. We aim to characterize the magnetic activity, rotation, and atmospheric variability of the L3.5 dwarf LSPM J0036+1821 through a multiwavelength analysis. We conducted a long-term monitoring campaign combining radio observations from the Very Large Array and very long baseline interferometry arrays with TESS optical photometry, spanning 2019-2023. We analyzed time-series data to derive precise rotation periods and constructed light curves and radio dynamic spectra. The radio lightcurves were modeled using an auroral framework analogous to Jupiter's magnetosphere, incorporating main oval and active field line components. We detect persistent, compact radio emission with significant variability and high circular polarization. Periodogram analysis yields a rotation period of 3.07941 $\pm$ 0.00020h from radio data, consistent with the TESS optical period (3.07908 $\pm$ 0.00038h). The radio light curves display stable morphology characterized by asymmetric, rotationally modulated pulses. Our modeling reproduces the emission using a hybrid auroral scenario, with the main oval contributing a stable component and an active field line producing dominant, variable bursts. Short-term variability is explained by small changes in emission cone geometry or longitude of the active field line. The radio emission of LSPM J0036+1821 is consistent with auroral processes driven by the electron cyclotron maser instability in a structured magnetosphere. The behavior is consistent with a Jupiter-like auroral system, possibly involving companion-driven or internally-driven magnetospheric interactions analogous to those observed in the Solar System.

Searching for hot water world candidates with CHEOPS: II. A low-density super-Earth orbiting the K dwarf TOI-2211

J. A. Egger, P. Cortés-Zuleta, D. Kubyshkina, H. P. Osborn et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用TESS和CHEOPS光度测量结合HARPS、ESPRESSO和KPF径向速度数据，精确刻画了环绕K型星TOI-2211的低密度超地球TOI-2211 b的质量和半径，并通过大气逃逸分析评估其挥发性包层的稳定性。研究表明TOI-2211 b是一个有前景的热水世界候选体，同时在径向速度数据中识别出一个外部非凌星行星候选体。该工作为检验行星形成和演化理论提供了重要观测约束，并通过行星形成演化生成模型重建了该多行星系统的完整结构。
英文原摘要
Small exoplanets on close-in orbits are exposed to high levels of stellar irradiation, which can effectively remove low-mass primordial H/He envelopes through atmospheric escape. Under these conditions, planets whose masses and radii are incompatible with both bare rocky interiors and extended H-dominated atmospheres are therefore expected to host high mean molecular weight atmospheres, making them promising candidates for volatile-rich hot water worlds. We present the first detailed characterisation of the low-density super-Earth TOI-2211 b (P~3.09 d), a recently validated transiting planet orbiting a K type star (G mag: 9.1). Combining TESS and CHEOPS photometry with radial velocity measurements from HARPS, ESPRESSO, and KPF, we precisely constrain the planet's radius and mass to $1.457 \pm 0.047$ R$_\oplus$ and $2.69 \pm 0.56$ M$_\oplus$. We also identify an outer, non-transiting planetary candidate (P~15.6 d, Msin(i) $ = 8.53 \pm 1.04$ M$_\oplus$) in the radial velocity data. We investigate the internal structure of the transiting planet and assess the stability of the inferred volatile envelopes through a detailed evaporation analysis, thereby identifying TOI-2211 b as a promising hot water world candidate. TOI-2211 b therefore joins the small but growing sample of these close-in low-density super-Earths, which represent particularly interesting targets for testing current planet formation and evolution theories. Additionally, we investigate the full architecture of the planetary system using a generative model of planet formation and evolution, with the resulting posterior distributions showing a clear over-density consistent with the observed orbital period and minimal mass of the non-transiting planetary candidate identified in the radial velocity data.
atmosphere · atmospheric · atmospheric escape

Solar Soft X-Ray Coronal Dimming in a Failed Eruption Associated with Plasma Cooling

Xinyue Wang, Astrid M. Veronig, Hechao Chen, Hui Tian · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究调查了一次M8.8级太阳耀斑伴随失败日珥喷发引发的异常日冕减暗事件，采用多波段观测（包括软X射线、极紫外和高温铁离子谱线）和微分辐射测量分析方法，发现该减暗主要源于活动区内部高温等离子体（>5 MK）向低温冷却，而非传统的日冕物质抛射导致的等离子体逃逸。研究表明深度热X射线减暗可在无显著日冕物质抛射的情况下发生，为恒星减暗现象提供了除日冕物质抛射外的替代物理机制，对理解恒星和日星的动态过程具有重要启示。
英文原摘要
Abstract Coronal dimmings are observed as sudden and localized reductions in the extreme-ultraviolet and X-ray emission of the solar corona. Traditionally, significant dimmings of spectral lines formed at temperatures of 1–2 MK are regarded as indicators of coronal mass ejections (CMEs), reflecting the density depletion caused by plasma escaping into interplanetary space. In this Letter, we report a peculiar deep coronal dimming event predominantly observed in high-temperature spectral lines following an M8.8-class confined solar flare associated with a failed filament eruption. Sun-as-a-star measurements from the Geostationary Operational Environmental Satellite and the Extreme Ultraviolet Variability Experiment reveal intensity reductions exceeding 30% in soft X-ray (SXR) and measurable decreases in Fe xviii (6.5 MK) and Fe xx (9.3 MK). Spatially resolved observations from the Atmospheric Imaging Assembly demonstrate that the dimming originates from the active region core, while the Solar Terrestrial Relations Observatory-A shows no evidence for CME-driven mass loss. Differential emission measure analysis reveals plasma at temperatures >5 MK cooling into lower temperatures, supporting plasma cooling as the dominant contributor to the hot-band dimming rather than CME-associated plasma escape. This event demonstrates that deep hot SXR dimmings can occur without substantial CME-driven mass loss, suggesting that alternative physical mechanisms may also account for unresolved stellar dimmings in addition to the commonly inferred CME signatures.

SOPHIE and TESS characterization of fourteen TESS Objects of Interest: nine planetary systems and five low-mass stellar companions

N. Heidari, G. Hébrard, S. Sousa, T. Forveille et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过结合TESS光度测量、SOPHIE径向速度、地面测光和高分辨率成像，对14个TESS关注天体进行了表征，确认了9个行星系统和5个低质量恒星伴星。行星样本包括3个热木星、6个温木星，其中TOI-6842A b是超低密度行星（密度0.08 g/cm³）且具有极佳的大气表征潜力，TOI-7025 b拥有单星行星中最大离心率（e=0.82）之一，TOI-5217A系统包含一颗外层伴星。这些发现为热木星膨胀模型和行星形成理论提供了重要的低辐照基准样本。
英文原摘要
We present fourteen TESS Objects of Interest (TOIs) from the SOPHIE-TESS follow-up program. Combining TESS photometry, SOPHIE radial velocities (RVs), ground-based photometry, and high-resolution imaging, we establish the nature of all targets. Nine systems (TOI-6457, TOI-6883A, TOI-6649, TOI-6842A, TOI-5217A, TOI-6893A, TOI-7025, TOI-7176, and TOI-5893) host planets. TOI-6649 b, TOI-6842A b, and TOI-5217A b are transiting hot Jupiters with periods of 4.4-6.0 d, radii of 1.3-1.6 RJ, masses of 0.3-1.3 MJ, and densities of 0.1-0.6 g/cm3. TOI-5217A also hosts an outer companion, TOI-5217A c, with a period of about 1761 d, a minimum mass of about 13 MJ, and an eccentric orbit (e = 0.53 +/- 0.02). TOI-5893 b, TOI-6893A b, TOI-6457 b, TOI-7176 b, and TOI-7025 b are transiting warm Jupiters with periods of 40.4-67.0 d, radii of 0.8-1.2 RJ, and masses of 0.5-1.0 MJ. TOI-6883A b, a recently reported warm Jupiter, is further characterized with new photometry and SOPHIE and TRES RVs. The other five systems (TOI-5621AB, TOI-5321AB, TOI-3678AB, TOI-5906AB, TOI-5308AB) show large RV amplitudes indicating eclipsing binaries (companions of 0.1-0.6 Msun and 0.1-0.6 Rsun). TOI-6842A b is a super-puff (density 0.08 +/- 0.01 g/cm3), among the nine lowest-density planets known, and with a transmission spectroscopy metric of about 250 it is an excellent target for atmospheric characterization. The six warm Jupiters, with equilibrium temperatures of 377-846 K, are low-irradiation benchmarks for hot-Jupiter inflation models. TOI-7025 b has an eccentricity of 0.82 +/- 0.02, one of the largest known for a planet orbiting a single star. Finally, using Gaia astrometric excess noise, we detect TOI-5906 B, the first object identified by transit, RV, and astrometry.

Enhancing the Interpretability of Radial Velocity Analysis Using Leave-one-out Cross-validation

Matthew C. Nixon, Ritvik Basant, Rafael Luque, Luis Welbanks et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过留一法交叉验证(LOO-CV)增强径向速度(RV)系外行星检测分析的可解释性,用于诊断异常数据点和进行模型选择。通过HD 119130 b、GJ 4276和GJ 357三个系统的案例分析,证明LOO-CV能够识别主导异常结果的个别测量值、区分竞争模型以及评估高斯过程噪声模型中的行星信号,为未来高精度RV巡天的行星探测提供实用指导。
英文原摘要
Radial velocity (RV) observations are reaching increasingly high precision, enabling the targeting of low-amplitude signals. Assessing results obtained at the limits of our capabilities requires understanding the relationship between data, models and inference. Leave-one-out cross-validation (LOO-CV) has become an increasingly popular tool for interpretable model criticism in astronomical data analysis. In this work, we demonstrate how LOO-CV can provide insight into the RV analysis of exoplanetary systems through three case studies. We first investigate the anomalously high mass of HD 119130 b inferred from CARMENES RVs, later revised significantly downward after follow-up observations with other instruments. We show that this high mass was strongly driven by two individual measurements, whose removal significantly reduces the tension between data sets. Next, we use LOO-CV as a model selection tool for the GJ 4276 system. Here, LOO-CV prefers an eccentric single-planet model over a two-planet model, and identifies the orbital phases at which follow-up observations would best discriminate between them. Finally, we extend LOO-CV to models with correlated noise, applying it to the GJ 357 system, whose RVs are modeled with a Gaussian process. We find no predictive support for a fourth planetary signal that is moderately favored by the Bayesian evidence, strengthening the interpretation of a three-planet architecture previously reported in the literature. We provide practical guidance for implementing LOO-CV in RV analysis, together with an online tutorial. Such methods will become ever more important as future RV surveys push the limits of planet detection and characterization.
radial velocity

Detecting multiple planets in Gaia data using Bayesian model selection

Emmanuel J. Greenfield, James E. Owen · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究调查了Gaia对单行星和双行星系统的探测能力，重点关注2:1平均运动共振附近的系统，并采用贝叶斯模型选择来区分多行星信号。研究发现，当用单行星模型拟合圆形双行星共振系统时，会产生显著的参数偏差（离心率偏差可达0.8、质量偏差100%、倾角偏差50°），这些偏差在行星测量特征相似时最为严重。虽然许多双行星系统可通过贝叶斯模型选择正确识别，但低信噪比的行星可能被隐藏，导致外行星轨道参数的系统性偏差；利用轨道近似和近日点进动观测可在某些情况下打破简并，但这些参数偏差可能对Gaia星族群体统计约束行星形成、迁移和演化模型产生重要影响。
英文原摘要
Gaia will soon provide a census of cold giant planets. However, an astrometric signal contains the superimposed reflex motion of all companions, so planetary signals cannot be treated individually. We therefore investigate Gaia's planet-detection capabilities for 1-planet and 2-planet systems, with special attention to systems near 2:1 mean-motion resonance. Gaia can detect 2-planet systems, although the lower-S/N planet requires a higher S/N threshold, especially when the outer planet is more massive and near 2:1 commensurability. When fitting a 1-planet model to a circular 2-planet resonant system, the resulting fit shows biases in eccentricity (up to e = 0.8), mass (up to 100%), and inclination (up to 50 deg). The bias is maximised when the planets' astrometric signatures are similar, since the combined orbital signal deviates most from a single Keplerian orbit. Many of these systems can be identified as having 2 planets using Bayesian model selection. Still, in some cases the second planet is hidden and undetectable due to its S/N, leading to these biases for the outer planet's orbital parameters. When the outer planet is roughly over three times as massive as the inner planet, orbital approximations allow us to recover the astrometric resoeccentric degeneracy from the bias. When the planets are in dynamical resonance, apsidal precession observed over a long enough baseline (e.g. DR5) can contribute to breaking the degeneracy at short orbital periods. Overall, these biases might significantly affect constraints on planet formation, migration, and evolution models from Gaia population-wide demographics.

First Direct Identification of a Multi-Star Microlens System Hosting a Planet

Sean K. Terry, David P. Bennett, Jean-Philippe Beaulieu, T. Dex Bhadra et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过凯克自适应光学和哈勃太空望远镜的高分辨率成像,首次直接探测到微引力透镜事件OGLE-2006-BLG-284中双星透镜系统的光学信号,并结合光变曲线参数精确测量了透镜-源相对自行运动(5.80±0.15 mas yr⁻¹)。结合多波段测光数据,研究确定了两颗主透镜星质量(M₁=0.43±0.05 M☉, M₂=0.12±0.02 M☉)和行星伴星质量(mₚ=165⁺⁴⁰₋₃₃ M⊕),为微引力透镜探测多星系统行星提供了直接观测证据。
英文原摘要
Nearly 25 years have passed since gravitational microlensing delivered its first exoplanet discoveries. The relative proper motion between lens and source systems allows them to be resolved as they separate on the sky over many years. This enables scientists to study light coming directly from the lenses and place tight constraints on the physical properties of these planetary systems. In this paper we present an analysis of high angular resolution images of the microlensing target OGLE-2006-BLG-284 using Keck adaptive optics and HST data taken more than 15 years after the event. We clearly detect the lens system and measure its motion across the Keck and HST epochs. We measure a lens-source relative proper motion of $5.80 \pm 0.15$ mas yr$^{-1}$. A combination of the light curve parameters and the host star(s) brightnesses in $V,\, I$, and $K$ passbands gives primary and secondary host star masses of $M_{\rm 1} = 0.43 \pm 0.05\,M_{\odot}$ and $M_{\rm 2} = 0.12 \pm 0.02\,M_{\odot}$, and a planetary companion mass of $m_p = 165^{+40}_{-33}\,M_{\oplus}$. Although we are unable to determine if the planet orbits one or both stars in the system, our analysis motivates next-generation high-precision RV measurements that can distinguish these two possibilities. This is the first direct detection of flux coming from a two-star microlensing system which hosts a planet.

An Open Cluster Origin for Most Solar-type Binaries in the Solar Neighborhood and Implications for Primordial Planet Stability

Anna C. Childs, Aaron M. Geller · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用Gaia DR3数据、星团形成演化模型和N体模拟，定量估计了过去5 Gyr内来自动力学演化过的疏散星团的太阳型双星对邻近星场双星群体的贡献，发现至少53%的太阳型双星起源于初始质量≥200 M⊙的星团。通过直接N体模拟和经验拟合，研究定量刻画了恒星相互作用对环双星和环单星行星稳定性的影响，给出了双星分离度与环双星行星失稳的关系以及稳定环单星共面相空间的约化比例。这些结果为评估疏散星团动力学演化对双星系统行星稳定性的影响提供了框架，有助于解释观测到的系外行星统计特性。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present the first Gaia-calibrated estimate of the contribution of dynamically processed open clusters (OCs) to the nearby solar-type field binary population and investigate the implications for primordial planet stability. We combine Gaia DR3 observations, empirical models of cluster formation and dissolution, and direct N -body simulations to constrain the fraction of field FGK-type binaries within 1 kpc that originated in OCs over the past 5 Gyr. We find that at least ∼53% of solar-type field binaries formed in OCs with initial masses ≥200 M ⊙ , where dynamical processing becomes significant. We quantify the effects of stellar encounters and binary orbital evolution on the stability of both circumbinary and circumstellar planets by deriving empirical prescriptions for circumbinary planet destabilization as a function of binary separation and for the fractional reduction of stable circumstellar coplanar phase space. These results provide a framework for evaluating how OC dynamical evolution may influence the stability of planets in binary systems and therefore contribute to observed exoplanet demographics.

Accretion Geometry and Variability in QS Telescopii from multi-epoch Observations

L Booi, S B Potter, Z N Khangale, V Moloi et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究利用多个地基和空间望远镜对极星QS Telescopii进行了2017-2025年期间的多波段观测,首次在该源中检测到2-11分钟的准周期振荡(QPOs),使其成为仅有的七颗显示此现象的极星之一。通过多普勒层析成像,详细刻画了电离氢和He II发射线的三维结构,揭示了来自次星、弹道流、磁场限制区等多个区域的复杂吸积流动过程。圆偏振光谱显示±10%至+25%的偏振变化和两个与~53 MG磁场强度相符的回旋共振驼峰,这些结果显著改进了对该极星吸积几何和磁场结构的理解。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present phase-resolved photometry, spectroscopy, photopolarimetry, and circular spectropolarimetry of the polar QS Telescopii, obtained between 2017 and 2025 using the South African Astronomical Observatory (SAAO) 1.0-m and 1.9-m telescopes, the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS), Asteroid Terrestrial-impact Last Alert System (ATLAS) and the Southern African Large Telescope (SALT). From ground-based photometry, we report the first detection of quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) in QS Tel, with timescales ranging between 2 and 11 minutes. This makes QS Tel one of only seven polars to show QPOs. The average spectra of QS Tel show broad and strong Balmer ($\rm H\delta$, $\rm H\gamma$, $\rm H\beta$ and $\rm H\alpha$) and He ii 4686 Å emission lines, along with weaker emission from He i at (6678 Å, 5017 Å, 4921 Å, 4712 Å, 4471 Å, and 4387 Å) and the Bowen fluorescence complex C iii/N iii at 4650 Å. For the first time detailed Doppler tomography of the $\rm H\alpha$ and He ii 4686 Å lines are presented which reveal emission from the irradiated face of the secondary star, the ballistic stream, the threading region and complex magnetically confined flows. The magnetically confined accretion stream splits into two distinct components coming from the same stream. One component appears near the threading region and the other component is seen along the high velocity in the magnetically confined accretion stream closer to the White Dwarf. Broad band photopolarimetry reveals circular polarisation reaching levels of +8%, however more detailed circular spectropolarimetry shows polarisation ranging from −10% to +25%, associated with two broad, phase-dependent cyclotron humps centred at ~4800 Å and ~6300 Å, consistent with a magnetic field strength of ~53 MG.

行星形成与盘演化2 篇

Reflex instabilities II: The reflex motion of the central object drives at least seven instabilities in accretion discs

Nathan Magnan, Michel Tagger, Clément Baruteau, Aurélien Crida et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过线性稳定性分析,系统研究了中心天体在吸积盘自引力作用下的反冲运动对盘层稳定性的影响,发现并分类了七种不同的不稳定性模式。其中仅SLING不稳定性和三种新的"空腔"不稳定性可能对真实吸积盘产生物理影响,可用WKB方法精确描述,但其余三种人工不稳定性在数值模拟中难以避免,会严重偏置对偏心率增长、行星迁移和盘结构的观测预测,表明当前数值模拟工具在处理中心天体反冲运动方面存在显著缺陷。
英文原摘要
This series of papers studies the stability of accretion discs to the reflex motion of the central object under the gravitational pull of the disc itself. In this second paper, we use linear analysis to reproduce or discover seven distinct instabilities. We outline their defining characteristics and propose a classification. Of the four instabilities that had already been discovered, three are artificial: one does not survive the transition to modern surface density profiles such as power-laws, one is due to the location of the domain's boundaries, and one is due to the use of damping layers near the domain's boundaries. Only the SLING instability of Adams et al. (1989) and the three new 'cavity' instabilities seem capable of affecting accretion discs. All four can be described to a high degree of precision by WKB methods. They may drive eccentricity growth, affect planet migration, create spiral and crescent substructures in the absence of any planet, and boost accretion onto the central object. The issue is that the three artificial instabilities are extremely hard to avoid in simulations, and would bias the same observables. This suggests that our community may not have the tools to model the reflex motion of the central object yet.
planet migration

Just a Phase? Weakening Vertical Shear Instability Explains Class II Disk Morphologies: Simulations with Dust Coagulation, Sedimentation, Thermal Relaxation, and Backreaction

Thomas Pfeil, Alexandros Ziampras, Shangjia 尚嘉 Zhang 张, Philip J. Armitage et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究通过包含尘埃凝聚、沉降、热弛豫和反馈的原行星盘模拟,揭示了竖直剪切不稳定性(VSI)的演化特征。研究发现,当考虑尘埃沉降驱动的凝聚和气-尘热耦合效应时,VSI诱导的强湍流在几十万年内被抑制,盘结构从垂直伸展演化为观测到的薄盘形态,与主流Class II盘的观测特征相符。这一结果表明VSI可能主要活跃于Class I阶段,为解释原行星盘形态演化提供了新的物理框架。
英文原摘要
Abstract The vertical shear instability (VSI) is known to create turbulence and strong vertical mixing in protoplanetary disks if thermal relaxation is sufficiently fast. In simulations where this condition is met, VSI can loft dust particles to large altitudes, creating a vertically extended appearance in mock millimeter-wavelength observations, which is inconsistent with the morphology of the majority of observed Class II disks. We present simulations of protoplanetary disks with VSI that are consistent with the observed thin-disk geometries, while maintaining the commonly observed bowl-shaped morphology in scattered light images at micrometer wavelengths. We show that this outcome arises naturally when the effects of dust coagulation, sedimentation, and dust–gas thermal accommodation are taken into account. Sedimentation-driven coagulation removes large amounts of dust from the disk atmosphere, in the process slowing down the dust-driven cooling of the gas. At the same time, a dense midplane layer of millimeter-sized grains forms, which exerts aerodynamic drag on the gas. This results in the termination of the VSI’s corrugation mode. Only weak VSI activity remains in the upper and lower hemispheres. These processes occur on the typical dust growth timescale and suppress strong VSI-induced turbulence within a few hundred thousand years. VSI could thus generally be restricted to the Class I evolutionary stages of protoplanetary disks.

Ariel Tier 3 Observations: An Opportunity to Explore both Stellar and Planetary Variability through Broad Wavelength Time-domain Spectroscopy

Alexandra H. Thompson, Quentin Changeat, Theresa Luftinger, Giovanna Tinetti · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究调查了Ariel任务第3层观测策略中恒星变率对行星大气特征提取的影响,采用模拟重复观测和不同复杂度的三种缓解方法进行对比分析。研究发现强恒星活动会导致简单叠加观测数据时产生显著系统偏差,但通过渐进式光学数据剔除或单次访问联合反演修正可有效恢复行星参数,且各方法在精度和计算效率间存在权衡。这表明在Ariel多次观测数据分析中需重视恒星变率影响,但通过灵活采用相应缓解策略辅以恒星监测,仍可对活跃系统进行可靠的大气表征。
英文原摘要
Ariel's Tier 3 observing strategy offers a unique opportunity to explore planetary and stellar variability in the spectral and time domain. However, without the correct toolkit, temporal variability of the host star may complicate combined analysesof observations obtained at different epochs. We investigate the impact of stellar variability on Ariel Tier 3 observations and establish three different mitigation approaches of increasing physical complexity. Using simulated repeated observations of a favourable system contaminated by different spot and facula configurations, we compare a naive, unmodified stacking approach, separate analysis of the infrared region through progressive removal of the most strongly contaminated optical wavelengths, and the correction of individual visits using combined star-planet retrievals. We find that stacking inconsistent visits can produce substantial biases in the retrieved planetary properties under strong stellar variability, although a single activity correction can still recover the planetary parameters reasonably well. Applying progressive optical data cuts substantially improves the accuracy of retrievals up to a point, without requiring any in-retrieval correction and causing only a modest loss of precision. Correcting individual visits prior to stacking produces more consistent spectra and generally smaller uncertainties, but requires greater computational effort. These results demonstrate that stellar variability should be considered when interpreting repeated Ariel observations, but need not preclude atmospheric characterisation of active systems. A flexible suite of mitigation approaches, supported by preparatory and contemporaneous stellar monitoring, can allow the treatment of stellar variability to be adapted to individual targets while maintaining the efficiency required to maximise Ariel's scientific return.
